# Algorithm methodology follow-ups

**Status:** open review and implementation backlog. Accepted methodology is
identified in linked records; runtime-change authorization is not implied. **Audit:** 2026-11-05, source revision
`7e82fd03ad98346727769e88dd5ac5d84b56e9fc`.

This backlog consolidates **653 review items across 33 source scopes with open items**
in the [algorithm catalogue](README.md). Accepted methodology is identified
in the owning records; other pending decisions retain their labels.
Owning records provide formulas, source/test/reference evidence and scope
limits. The [central follow-ups](../FOLLOW_UPS.md#financial-methodology-audit)
retain cross-domain coordination.

This register owns mutable algorithm work. Records and inventories retain
formulas, observed behavior, material limitations and evidence qualifications,
and link here for action details. Each action has one authoritative entry; use
links for work owned by the central register. Record progress and completion
evidence with the owning entry instead of duplicating task lists in records.

The labels distinguish the work required:

- **Observed** (53): a static implementation, contract, dispatch or
  documentation difference. Its financial impact is not necessarily reproduced.
- **Investigate** (18): a source-derived numerical or economic concern
  requiring targeted reproduction, an independent reference or convergence
  study before a defect conclusion.
- **Decision** (38): a financial convention, supported domain, approximation,
  failure policy or result meaning requiring explicit agreement.
- **Reconciliation** (506): implementation and verification work against an
  accepted contract, without reopening that contract for adoption.
- **Evidence** (38): a particular primary-reference, empirical-calibration,
  independent-fixture or numerical-qualification gap.

These categories are not severity rankings. A decision can expose implementation
work, and an observed difference can require numerical investigation. Earlier
agreed directions remain implementation reconciliation requirements; they are
not reopened as optional choices. The [audit boundary](AUDIT_BOUNDARY.md) records
the source and evidence limits; [ADR 0026](governance/0026-methodology-decisions-and-inventory.md)
defines adoption and independent statuses.

Record closure in this register, linking to the agreed scope/rationale and
appropriate implementation or validation evidence in the owning methodology.
Applied documentation corrections are excluded unless an underlying policy or
runtime question remains. Shared-kernel issues appear at their owner; distinct
product consequences remain in their product scope.

## Core conventions

[Scope inventory](core-conventions/README.md).

- **Reconciliation — Accepted holiday engine and jurisdiction calendars.** The complete packages in [004](core-conventions/004-holiday-engine.md#accepted-decision-holiday-engine-and-data-boundaries) and [005](core-conventions/005-jurisdiction-calendars.md#accepted-decision-jurisdiction-calendar-purposes-and-coverage) were accepted on 2026-11-07. Their common engine contract and 26-retained/2-retired purpose matrix, forecast permissions and historical/annual-data boundaries are not reopened. Reconciled on 2026-11-08: `HolidayCalendar` membership and counting are fallible (`Result`) and errors propagate through core adjustment/search, Bus252, cashflow and valuation callers and both hosts; effective ranges gate the nominal event year once with spillovers retained; contributing nominal years are derived from each rule's occurrence window (no fixed year±1 probe, no prior-year rematerialization); transform arithmetic is checked; invalid ordinals, impossible month/days, zero-length spans, multi-occurrence bridge festivals and non-positive Easter years are errors; missing lunar/equinox table years error while irrelevant neighbouring years do not; failed years are never cached; reversed business-day counts error. Remaining engine work is strict JSON/CSV admission and coverage metadata (004 items 8, 10, 11). The Frankfurt/Hesse correction and the dated NSW Anzac additions landed 2026-11-13 with pinned sources. Implement the SIFMA full/early-close distinctions, Indian exceptional notices, complete SSE annual notices and epoch-correct Japanese citizens rules. The generic `cme` and `cnbe` calendars were removed without aliases on 2026-11-08; commodity forwards now require the contract's settlement calendar (no convention-inferred calendar) and reject `nerc` as a settlement calendar (owner resolution O3). Remove automatic SSE estimates/bridge selection, and constrain NERC and exchange IDs to their declared purposes in calendar metadata. Independently qualify closures and open dates, HK collision/2012 transitions, Japan substitutes/Olympics/equinox status, historical weekend limits, amendments and each advertised source range. Partial India 2024–2026 moving rows and China 2004–2026 Spring/Labour spans are not whole-calendar qualification; Singapore's maintained moving window is 2024–2027 and Matariki ends in 2052. Preserve frozen provenance, forecast status and explicit errors outside admitted domains. Runtime implementation, data refresh and independent qualification remain pending.

- **Reconciliation — Accepted calendar-input admission contract.** Reconciled on 2026-11-08 against [004's accepted input contract](core-conventions/004-holiday-engine.md#accepted-decision-holiday-engine-and-data-boundaries): the calendar generator rejects unknown calendar and rule fields recursively, requires `ignore_weekends`, every fixed rule's `observed` (including `none`) and every span's `offset` (all 28 definitions now state them), declares the optional rule `name`, and admits only 1..=32-byte lowercase ASCII IDs; the reference-data CSV readers reject surplus or missing cells. Runtime evaluation (C1b) now errors on invalid ordinals, impossible month/days and zero-length spans. Remaining: move those semantic date/nth-weekday/span/effective-bound checks into generator admission so a malformed definition fails the build, declare each table's range separately from the 1970–2150 cache bounds, and independently qualify malformed and boundary inputs; a passing renderer check does not validate financial inputs.

- **Reconciliation — Accepted named CDS contractual date regimes.** Owner resolution O7 was accepted on 2026-11-12 in [CDS materialization](calibration-framework/07-cds-materialization.md#accepted-decision-named-cds-contractual-date-regimes) and [CDS legs](models-and-valuations-credit/12-cds-legs.md#accepted-decision-named-cds-contractual-date-regimes), within [008's accepted schedule contract](core-conventions/008-schedule-generation.md#accepted-decision-schedule-grids-events-and-failure-boundaries) (generic CDS mode and start substitution removed on 2026-11-11, E4) and [009's semiannual lattices](core-conventions/009-contractual-roll-dates.md#accepted-decision-unadjusted-roll-dates-and-product-boundaries). On 2026-11-12 (E4b-2) calibration's tenor maturity became the post-2015 single-name rule (Mar/Sep 20 roll on or before the trade date, plus 3M, plus the tenor; positive whole-quarter tenors only), pinned to the 2015-12-22 3M/9M fixtures, and explicit maturities became literal. On 2026-11-12 (E4b-3/4) `RollRule::CdsImm` and every cashflows CDS branch were removed; CDS premium legs, tranches and the base-correlation step carry a required product-owned `CdsDateRegime` (`standard` CDS roll dates or `bespoke`, no default); the standard regime pays quarterly from an accrual start on a roll date and owns its stub (short final period for an off-grid maturity), so the pricer's short-front check is gone; a standard tranche quote's maturity is literal and must be a roll date; schemas, Python, WASM, parity goldens, calibration examples 05 and 12 and notebooks use `date_regime`. Remaining: the five dates are separate product fields (premium start/end, protection effective date and valuation convention, upfront date, protection settlement delay) but not yet resolved by one product-owned function per regime. Independently qualify other tenors, windows and regimes against dated official sources before admitting them. Records: [CDS materialization](calibration-framework/07-cds-materialization.md#accepted-decision-named-cds-contractual-date-regimes), [CDS legs](models-and-valuations-credit/12-cds-legs.md), [008](core-conventions/008-schedule-generation.md), [009](core-conventions/009-contractual-roll-dates.md), [CDS-option quadrature](models-and-valuations-credit/15-cds-option-quadrature.md).

- **Evidence — 30/360 family qualification.** [003's full package](core-conventions/003-thirty360.md#accepted-decision-synthetic-day-count-conventions) was reconciled on 2026-11-09: five explicit `DayCount` selections including the new `Thirty360Isda` (`30_360_isda`, ISDA bond basis), an optional termination status that 30E/360 ISDA requires explicitly (equal dates included), signed reversal that keeps the later endpoint's status, exact integer numerators, and removal of the raw `days_30_360`/`days_30e_360_isda`/`Thirty360Convention` exports and host wrappers. The QuantLib fixture was regenerated by running QuantLib 1.43 with exact day counters and termination inputs recorded (`core/tests/golden/generate_daycount_quantlib.py`); its `certified` flag, wrong leap-February description and alias claims are gone. Remaining: qualify, independently of that one vendor, the February/31st/leap/termination, signed/equal, malformed-context, synthetic-zero and nonadditivity cases. Product mapping of contractual selection stays with the product records. Records: [003](core-conventions/003-thirty360.md).

- **Reconciliation — Accepted FX quote metadata and value-date boundaries.** [011's complete FX quote/date package was accepted on 2026-11-07](core-conventions/011-fx-date-and-quote-conventions.md#accepted-decision-explicit-fx-quote-metadata-and-value-dates); its ordered explicit metadata, reciprocal, inference removals and forward-value-date choices are not reopened. Reconcile FX spot/forward/swap/NDF and XCCY calibration callers as a working slice, including actual/owned spot dates, orientation-matched pips and product-payload defaults; do not retain private heuristic copies or add a generic rules engine. Implement explicit-calendar Natural/conditional M/Y business-EOM branches, open-spot/open-result/strictly-later admission and necessary-data/error boundaries. Propagate removed factories/enums/fields/helpers and new canonical inputs across Rust/hosts/state/schema/parity/examples. Independently qualify quote units/reciprocals, closed/collapsed dates, business versus calendar EOM, fully closed preserved months, Natural cross-month rolls, required coverage/date limits and host errors. Actual pair/product/venue conventions and source editions remain separately owned; dated EBS/ANZ references do not certify universal present-day defaults. Runtime reconciliation landed 2026-11-13: validated explicit `FxPairConvention`, the removed factories/enum/spot helpers, `fx_forward_date` with the Natural and conditional business-EOM branches and its admission errors, and the five callers on an explicit joint-calendar spot rule (stated lag and both calendars, no pair table, implicit USD gate or weekends fallback); `FxSpot` uses an explicit settlement date as given and loses its business-day convention. Independent product/current-market qualification, and any product that needs a CLS-style USD-intermediate exception selecting it, remain pending. Record: [011](core-conventions/011-fx-date-and-quote-conventions.md).

- **Reconciliation — Accepted schedule grid, event and failure contract.** [008's complete schedule-generation package was accepted on 2026-11-07](core-conventions/008-schedule-generation.md#accepted-decision-schedule-grids-events-and-failure-boundaries). On 2026-11-09 the failure contract's first part landed: `ScheduleErrorPolicy`, `ScheduleWarning`, `GracefulEmpty`, the missing-calendar warning, `Schedule.warnings`/`has_warnings`/`used_graceful_fallback` and the monotonic/drop repair are removed from Rust, Python and WASM, and a non-increasing generated grid is an error. On 2026-11-11 (E3b) the rest of the core contract landed: a required `GridMode` in place of the mode booleans, a complete `ScheduleSpec` with no frequency/stub/grid/convention defaults and `start < end`, strict on-grid EOM/IMM regular anchors with no replacement search or implicit stub, strict calendar resolution and calendar requirements, the final-date ceiling, private validated `Schedule` state and `ScheduleSpec::nominal_reference`, across Rust, Python, WASM, schema, parity and examples. On 2026-11-11 (E4) the generic CDS mode went: `ScheduleBuilder::cds_imm`, `ScheduleSpec.cds_imm_mode` and the prior-roll start substitution are removed from Rust, Python and WASM; the cashflows `RollRule::CdsImm` keeps the leg's own frequency and stub, requires its regular anchor (start for back stubs, maturity for front stubs) on a CDS roll date and anchors only interior coupon-window cuts on the adjacent roll before clipping; the standard tranche states its short-back stub; the reference-period `.ok()?` became an error; and the CDS-option and tranche valuation-date start fallbacks are errors. The CDS pricer states its own grid anchor until E4b. Remaining: the complete CDS contractual date regime (E4b). Reconcile CDS/accrual-adjustment callers with product owners; independently qualify stubs, direction-specific clamping, date/resource limits, calendar failures, lag ordering, collisions, nominal references and restored states. Actual product accrual/observation convention selection remains separately owned. Runtime work and independent qualification remain pending. Record: [008](core-conventions/008-schedule-generation.md).

- **Reconciliation — Accepted reporting/fiscal periods, statistical factors and LTM completeness.** [014's complete period/LTM package](core-conventions/014-fiscal-periods-ltm.md#accepted-decision-reporting-periods-fiscal-context-and-ltm-windows) was accepted on 2026-11-07: exact Gregorian/ISO and actual-opening fiscal partitions/labels, removal of fiscal defaults/country presets, explicit retained PeriodCalendar context, strict signed grammar/ranges/actuals, checked contextual stepping, structural total ordering, statistical-only annualization_factor and synthetic-prior removal, validated private period/plan state, reporting-slot and calendar-day LTM with weekly excluded, target presence and exact geometric missing counts distinct from data/value eligibility. Reconcile parser/Ord, Gregorian FY rejection, context-free fiscal paths, all state/hosts/schema/parity and actual analytics/statements/reporting consumers together; propagate malformed-window errors rather than masking them as availability. Independently qualify year0/negative/finite-domain boundaries, ISO/fiscal short weeks, opening/month-anchor clamping, source/result shifts, cutoff/prefix/range/resource rules, round-trip context and invalid state, total-order laws, daily/reporting LTM boundaries and incomplete versus malformed history. Runtime reconciliation landed 2026-11-12/13: structural `(year, rank, index, fiscal)` ordering with statements models holding one period kind, the exact signed grammar with Gregorian-builder `FY` rejection and checked bounds, fallible `annual`, removal of `FiscalConfig::default` and the country presets, a public `PeriodCalendar` retained on plans, models and results, context-aware `shift(periods, calendar)` replacing `next_fiscal`/`prev_fiscal`, and `ltm_window(target, plan)` with target presence, weekly rejection, full calendar-day daily coverage and propagated window errors (statements `ttm()` and statements-analytics credit sums no longer mask them). The statistical factor is `annualization_factor` (risk scaling only) beside a calendar `slots_per_year`, `prior_observation_date` is removed, and statements `annualize`/`growth_rate` take explicit factors and lags (O8). Remaining for 014: private validated `Period`/`PeriodPlan` fields and independent qualification. [007's accepted tenor/date package](core-conventions/007-tenor-date-arithmetic.md#accepted-decision-tenor-and-date-arithmetic-conventions) is not reopened. Its core arithmetic was reconciled on 2026-11-09: representation-based count limits with checked month offsets, `to_years_approx` (renamed, no alias), exact integer `to_days_approx` (`6M` = 183), `payments_per_year` rejecting D/W (the Hull-White swaption reads it only for cash par-yield settlement, O5), a checked `date_from_epoch_days -> Result`, no WASM `i32` narrowing, and a tenor bridge that takes the caller's `DayCountContext` and rejects an end before the origin. `months_until` now returns `Result` with the original-anchor completed-month rule (reversed dates error), and `Tenor::add_to_date` rejects an omitted calendar unless the convention is `Unadjusted` (2026-11-09). `compounding_frequency` is removed (2026-11-12): per O6 ([bond spread](valuations-bonds/12-spread-quotes.md#accepted-decision-required-quote-compounding), [term-loan](valuations-other-fixed-income/09-term-loan-risk-yields.md#accepted-decision-required-quote-compounding), [revolver](valuations-other-fixed-income/12-revolver-quotes-risk.md#accepted-decision-required-quote-compounding), [borrowing-base](valuations-structured-credit/19-borrowing-base-facility.md#accepted-decision-required-quote-compounding)) Bond, TermLoan, RevolvingCredit and ABF each carry a required quote `Compounding` shared by spread pricing, inversion and CS01. Remaining for 007: removal of `from_years` (its ASW, annuity, scenario and structured-credit callers need contractual tenor metadata that `ForwardCurve` does not carry), the structured-credit month-count measures (each site's meaning is now stated in the [structured-credit README](valuations-structured-credit/README.md#month-counts-at-structured-credit-sites); all sites keep a named pre-007 count until their owning record selects a measure), and independent approximation/date qualification of the pricing/scenario/seasoning callers. Financial owners retain actual fiscal regimes, explicit observation factors, value/missing/summation/rounding and eligible additive-flow policies; no inferred business closure or 52-week accounting convention is adopted here. Records: [007](core-conventions/007-tenor-date-arithmetic.md), [014](core-conventions/014-fiscal-periods-ltm.md).

- **Reconciliation — Accepted monetary precision, rounding and currency identity.** [012's complete monetary/currency package](core-conventions/012-money-decimal-precision.md#accepted-decision-monetary-precision-rounding-and-currency-identity) was accepted on 2026-11-07 within [0020's accepted boundaries](conventions/0020-numerical-representation-and-rounding.md): one exact text/integer/Decimal/wire path; shortest-roundtrip-text float admission; exact-or-error sums; once-rounded bounded Decimal transforms with explicit half-quantum targets and overflow/nonzero-underflow errors; correctly rounded float views and direct dimensionless ratios; explicit financial scale/mode and precision-preserving absent ingest overrides; display-only defaults; Decimal-rate FX with exact-unit same-currency admission; alphabetic identity, unique-only numeric lookup and frozen currency provenance. Reconciled on 2026-11-08: one exact text grammar (`parse_decimal`, final value tested after the exponent), exact `DecimalWire` restoration with canonical normalized fixed-point output, exact Python integers and Money zero normalization. Reconciled on 2026-11-09: shortest-roundtrip-text float admission (item 4) in the one converter behind `Money::new` and the float adapters, rejecting values outside the Decimal domain; pricer model outputs use the single named `Money::from_model_value` adapter, which rounds that text half-even to the 28-digit scale (owner decision). Reconciled on 2026-11-09 (B3): Money addition/subtraction exact when representable and otherwise rounded only below the currency's minor unit, erroring if a minor-unit digit would be lost (owner decision D7 amending item 6); Decimal-scalar `checked_mul`/`checked_div` with qualified greatest-scale half-even rounding, nonzero underflow errors and named `_f64` adapters; a direct correctly-rounded binary64 `ratio`; Decimal-rate `convert_at_rate` with an exact same-currency unit rate and `convert_at_rate_f64`; Python/WASM twins. Reconciled on 2026-11-10 (B4): explicit-scale/mode `Money::round` (Python `round(scale, mode)` replacing `__round__`, WASM `round`), the `MidpointAwayFromZero` rename without an alias, no default `RoundingMode` with an optional policy/stamp mode, override-only ingest scales (absent means no ingest rounding; an override without a mode errors), fallible `from_decimal_with_config`, a 28-place formatting cap and sign-free displayed zero. Reconciled on 2026-11-10 (B5): alphabetic currency identity with position discriminants and a generated numeric/minor-unit metadata table (no enum casts), unique-only numeric lookup that names missing or ambiguous codes, a generator that admits shared numeric codes, and alphabetic `Currency` ordering for currency-keyed maps, SIMM output and Python hashing. The `amount()` binary64 view is not yet correctly rounded (item 5). Reconcile the remaining JS number/text paths and all affected financial callers together. Independently qualify exact and inexact scientific input, malformed/restored state, precision/range/cancellation/halfway/underflow boundaries, float and ratio budgets, conditional rounding, same-currency nonunit rates and numeric reuse (ANG/XCG532). Audit known XCG/ZWG omissions and supported historical metadata against pinned SIX sources (XCG and ZWG added 2026-11-13 against pinned SIX List One/Three and amendments 176/177; the selection's remaining scope choices, such as omitting SVC, stay with the owner); no automatic redenomination/status engine. Required numerical guarantees remain targets until implementation and independent qualification, not established dependency behavior. Contract-specific rounding stages/accuracy remain with their existing owners. Record: [012](core-conventions/012-money-decimal-precision.md).

- **Reconciliation — Accepted cashflow representation/admission and NPV boundaries.** [015's complete cash/balance/accrual package](core-conventions/015-cashflow-semantics.md#accepted-decision-cash-settlement-principal-effects-and-accrual-metadata) was accepted on 2026-11-07: amount always signed settlement cash, zero-cash PIK/write-downs, explicit paired principal delta/date for every principal-like row including deliberate zero, literal/exhaustive kind meanings with Floating/ProtectionPayment and coupon/repayment-like classifications, distinct clocks, complete single-slice DayCountContextState, literal zero/signed scalar metadata, complete immutable validated construction/restoration and faithful cash-versus-event views. Reconciled on 2026-11-11 (G5): renamed/new kinds, zero-cash PIK/write-downs, required paired principal delta/date with every inference path removed (opening-balance heuristics, par-from-cash repayment, PIK interest readers), ProtectionPayment protection rows, margin substitutions and call dates, complete single-slice accrual context with literal factors, principal-date exports, and private validated `CashFlow`/`CashFlowAccrual` construction, update and restoration across Rust, Python, WASM, schemas and parity. Remaining: the #8 accrual fallbacks (accrued-interest reference period and frequency, the statements residual's synthesized accrual, the LSMC calendar fill), the inflation-linked bond's indexed-cash principal delta and the LSMC static premium redemption, each an owner choice for CASHFLOWS-001/013. Independently qualify cash/balance roll-forwards, explicit zero/opening funding, premiums/corrections/date lags, every kind, required context/long stubs, nonfinite/zero rates/factors, atomic updates and exact event round trips. Schedule calculation order, financial formula/PIK eligibility/recovery allocation and full product qualification remain separately owned. **016's complete package was also accepted on 2026-11-07:** [the discounting/NPV contract](core-conventions/016-discounting.md#accepted-decision-dated-npv-selection-arithmetic-and-continuous-discounting) requires explicit include_today/as_of, full currency/finiteness validation before selection, Money-empty error versus scalar-empty zero, no unused curve queries, source-owned dated relative factors, strict positive-factor/numeric failures, literal 012 Money conversion/products/exact sums and ordered checked scalar Neumaier arithmetic. It retains continuous flat discounting with positive finite output, permits negative rates/subnormal factors and rejects zero underflow; no universal scalar/exp accuracy guarantee or implicit curve currency is adopted. Reconciled on 2026-11-10 (G4): explicit `as_of`/`include_today` NPV paths with every row validated before selection, no source query when nothing nonzero is selected, relative `df_between_dates` factors, exact Money products/sums, checked scalar products and the shared checked Neumaier sum, `npv_amounts` rename, underflow-rejecting `flat_discount_factor`, explicit `include_today` in the valuations schedule helpers and removal of the private trade-NPV copy. Product same-day choices were kept as they were (holder views exclude same-day cash; the deposit's T+0 trade NPV includes it); a per-product review of those choices and of Monte Carlo/host behavior remains. Independently qualify same-day/zero/invalid excluded inputs, curve normalization/calendar/coverage overrides, Money factor and precision domains, scalar cancellation/subnormal/overflow and flat exponential extremes against independent references and owner accuracy budgets. The accepted NPV choices are not reopened; complete numerical/product qualification remains separate from 015's row/filter contract. Records: [015](core-conventions/015-cashflow-semantics.md), [016](core-conventions/016-discounting.md). Endpoint wording was clarified on2026-11-08 with [CASHFLOWS-001](cashflows/01-schedule-periods.md#accepted-decision-contractual-periods-calendars-and-day-count-context): actual calculation dates include explicitly selected accrual adjustment; nominal references remain separate and day-count/single-context rules unchanged.

- **Reconciliation — Accepted IRR/XIRR stream, clock, solver and numerical acceptance.** [017's complete IRR/XIRR package](core-conventions/017-irr.md#accepted-decision-irr-and-xirr-timing-eligibility-and-numerical-acceptance) was accepted on 2026-11-07: per-period original spacing, fixed ACT/365F dated effective annual returns, one canonical checked rounded net/date used consistently, one-switch eligibility in either direction, underdetermination/rate-domain errors, no public guess/generalized-clock inputs, full finite log bracket with direct rate endpoints, shared fallible Brent at100 iterations/1e-12 operational tolerance, and one finite returned-rate/residual<=1e-8 gate on every success path. Reconciled on 2026-11-10 (G1): signatures without guess or day count, fixed ACT/365F XIRR, one canonical per-date net, integer-offset periodic IRR, the full log bracket through a fallible shared `solve_in_bracket`, one acceptance gate on every success path, explicit loan `yield_basis` (ACT/365F/ACT/360 with exact growth conversion, O11), ACT/365F-only bond XIRR floors, basis-checked PE and real-estate IRRs, and removal of the Monte Carlo per-path IRR; the context-free-XIRR entry is closed. Independently qualify model netting/eligibility, cancellation/origin/zero spacing, leap timing, extreme rate endpoints, every exit path, finite residual and poor conditioning against exact/high-precision references. Operational residual/log tolerances do not certify uniform true rate error, ledger-exact netting, Excel root compatibility or scale/sign-flip success invariance. Preserve currency/sign/initial-terminal preparation, owner-specific reporting/candidate/yield policies and separate runtime/product qualification. Record: [017](core-conventions/017-irr.md).

- **Evidence — Accepted ratings and scorecards.** [019's complete package](core-conventions/019-credit-ratings.md#accepted-decision-generic-ratings-and-explicit-scorecard-scales) was accepted on 2026-11-07 and reconciled in runtime: `CreditRating` on 2026-11-09, the registry and scorecards on 2026-11-12 (embedded scales, `embedded_registry`, default score/scale and every unknown-scale fallback removed; caller-supplied registry with nonblank source and strict normalized ids/labels; `ScorecardScale::classify` with full `[0, 100]` coverage and representative self-membership; private validated construction and restore; scorecards snapshot the selected entry and reject unknown or repeated threshold/minimum labels in Rust, Python and WASM). Remaining: independent qualification of NR/incomparable/rank/alias/wire/style cases, normalized collisions, full-domain boundary classification, representative consistency, missing/malformed registry errors, invalid-state atomicity, snapshot replay and host errors. Metric formulas/weights/threshold gaps and ties/missing-factor rules, PD/transition/recovery/WARF data, NR collateral policies and regulatory/product eligibility remain with their owners. Record: [019](core-conventions/019-credit-ratings.md).

- **Evidence — Barrier observation and settlement qualification at each consumer.** [020's package](core-conventions/020-barrier-classification.md#accepted-decision-barrier-classification-touch-and-conditional-payment-timing) is reconciled in runtime: no enum defaults, rebate timing required exactly with a rebate, knock-in `AtHit` rejected, and enum wire/predicate tests complete. Independently qualify, at each actual consumer (analytic, Monte Carlo and PDE equity/FX barriers and FX touch), equality, adjacent-value and already-beyond observations; window, history and missing-data behavior; and conditional hit/expiry cash with its settlement and discount boundaries. Analytic/Monte Carlo/PDE monitoring, bridge/continuity corrections, rebate economics, model selection, Greeks and historical-settlement policy remain separately owned. Record: [020](core-conventions/020-barrier-classification.md).

- **Evidence — Astronomical calendar generation and source provenance.** The manual lunar generator uses unpinned lunar_python for seven CSVs, with Qing Ming UTC+8 and Japanese equinox UTC+9 adjustments. The active renderer only validates/emits maintained inputs; it performs no astronomical or official-data refresh. Establish pinned generator/environment/input/output revisions, the library civil-time contract, independent dated vectors including local-midnight boundaries, and CNY CSV provenance/reproducible maintenance. Distinguish published and projected ranges using actual publication editions, including Japanese Gazette evidence, rather than table presence or current wall-clock date. Remove unsupported cross-library/official-calibration claims; the existing digest and renderer check are not independent astronomical or holiday validation. Records: [004](core-conventions/004-holiday-engine.md), [005](core-conventions/005-jurisdiction-calendars.md).

- **Reconciliation — Accepted actual-day and coupon-context contracts.** [001's actual-day package](core-conventions/001-actual-day-counts.md#accepted-decision-actual-day-conventions) and [002's coupon-context package](core-conventions/002-coupon-context-day-counts.md#accepted-decision-coupon-context-day-counts) were accepted in full on 2026-11-07. 001's runtime work was reconciled on 2026-11-09: `DayCount::validate_context` validates structure then the selected convention's requirements before every equal-date zero (runtime contexts, `to_ctx` restoration and the analytics CAGR guard all use it), Bus252 rejects counts outside `0..=D`, ISDA adds an integer complete-year count to at most two partial years, AFB counts whole years in integers, and the inner equal-date shortcuts are gone; 001 still needs independent qualification of leap/century/date-limit, zero/reversed, malformed/restored context and cached/custom-calendar counts. 002's runtime work is reconciled: explicit context through the canonical tenor bridge and ACT/365L's coupon-end-year rule (without the annual exception or frequency requirement, with containment checked before equal-date zero and corrected A009 citations across source, hosts and the golden fixture) landed on 2026-11-09; on 2026-11-13 (E6) the inferred grids/month rounding/frequency-only shortcuts and 512-period walk were removed, `ActActIsma` enforces a contained nominal single-period `(period_start, period_end)` matching an explicit `{1M, 2M, 3M, 4M, 6M, 12M, 1Y}` frequency (checked before equal-date zero), `cashflows` and `valuations` own multi-period long-stub/quasi-coupon slicing, and Rust/Python/WASM/schema docs and the 7 ICMA golden cases are updated. Independently qualify leap/century/date-limit, zero/reversed, malformed/restored context, cached/custom-calendar counts, complete/short/long coupons, frequency mismatch, roll ambiguity, ACT/365L discriminators, partial-period additivity and endpoint-adjustment failures. These accepted choices are not reopened. Independent vendor qualification remains pending; instrument-specific selection retains its own owner.

- **Reconciliation — Accepted rolling/joint-calendar and roll-date contracts.** [006's rolling/joint-calendar package](core-conventions/006-business-day-adjustments.md#accepted-decision-rolling-and-joint-calendar-conventions) and [009's complete roll-date package](core-conventions/009-contractual-roll-dates.md#accepted-decision-unadjusted-roll-dates-and-product-boundaries) were accepted on 2026-11-07; their choices are not reopened. Reconciled for 006 on 2026-11-08: `BusinessDayConvention` has no `Default`, the cashflow schedule/fee and statements swap-convention wire forms require the convention, the Python/WASM tenor rolls require it, `+`-joined IDs with an empty member are rejected, and the Modified conventions compare year and month. The 100-day closed-day cap and `AdjustmentFailed.max_days` are removed: searches end only on a business day, the date-range end or a calendar error; Modified reverses without classifying the next month, Nearest stops once the distance and tie are proved, `Unadjusted` classifies nothing, and `add_business_days` steps uncapped (2026-11-09). Remaining for 006: remove `resolve_calendar(None)`'s weekends-only fallback and `resolve_joint_calendar`'s dropped-leg path together with the FX date callers (011), the empty-composite fallback with member-coverage intersection, and product-level instrument defaults such as valuations' `bdc_modified_following` (owned by each product); independently qualify ties/coverage, long closures, fully closed months, date extremes, offsets/counts, composite parsing and frozen restoration. For 009 (E5, 2026-11-12): the generic IMM option-expiry helpers were removed on 2026-11-08 and the private inclusive-IMM helpers with 008's schedule rewrite (E3b); the semiannual helpers' Rust, Python and WASM documentation now states only their lattice and inclusivity, and the tenor composition and first-accrual claims moved to the CDS product regime (E4b). The remaining 006 fallbacks (`resolve_calendar(None)`, `resolve_joint_calendar`'s single-leg and empty paths) go with the FX date callers in F5. Independently qualify equality, year transitions, membership, finite exhaustion, Good-Friday raw dates and host conversions. Actual product expiry/cutoff/session rules remain separately owned; CDS product decisions and affected composition remain in the existing CDS product-boundary entry. Runtime changes and independent qualification remain pending.

- **Reconciliation — Accepted SIFMA published-date, class and coverage contract.** [010's complete published-settlement package was accepted on 2026-11-07](core-conventions/010-sifma-settlement.md#accepted-decision-published-settlement-dates-classes-and-coverage); its choices are not reopened. Reconciled on 2026-11-08: explicit class everywhere (no Default, no Python/WASM default-class paths), checked `Result` exact lookup with not-found versus validation errors, class-specific strict-next search over at most the current and following months, removal of the business-day-index estimator, and fixed-date coverage/gap/equality tests replacing the rolling twelve-month test. Agency/term classification was also removed: `SifmaSettlementClass::from_agency_term` and the TBA/dollar-roll inference are gone and both products require `settlement_class` (owner resolution O2). Remaining: implement canonical Date-domain data admission in the generator; freeze source identity, edition, retrieval date, hash and per-class coverage beside `sifma_settlements.csv`; and independently qualify source-row extraction, gaps, month-end bypass, date extremes and frozen-edition replay. Actual product classification/settlement and separately selected projection models remain with their financial owners. Record: [010](core-conventions/010-sifma-settlement.md).

## Rates valuations

[Scope inventory](valuations-rates/README.md).

- **Observed — LMM sparse-ITM exercise uses future path information.** At ITM count<=basis_degree+3, the adapter chooses max(immediate, realized future chosen payoff), including out-of-sample mode. Qualify the proposed continue-only rule and exercise policy on held-out paths beyond MC standard error. [Record 09](valuations-rates/09-lmm-bermudans.md).

- **Observed — Bermudan contract gates vary by engine.** HW tree rejects NonCoTerminal; HW LSMC ignores the enum and values a common terminal maturity. LMM enforces physical/co-terminal schedules for future exercise but can price exercise today through HW before full validation. Reject unsupported contracts consistently or adopt actual implemented cashflows. Records: [08-HULL-WHITE-SWAPTIONS](valuations-rates/08-hull-white-swaptions.md), [09-LMM-BERMUDANS](valuations-rates/09-lmm-bermudans.md).

- **Observed — LMM controls and reported risks do not match all advertised inputs.** The adapter fixes two constant factors/common displacement max(0,1%-min forward), needs lmm_base_vol and ignores instrument mc_paths. Basis degrees alter thresholds without the expected regressors; HwSigmaVega is not base-vol risk and expected exercise time is not published. Reconcile controls and qualify parameter calibration. Records: [09-LMM-BERMUDANS](valuations-rates/09-lmm-bermudans.md).

- **Reconciliation — Accepted LMM Bermudan pricing, stopping and risk.** Record 09 was accepted on 2026-11-14 for exact physical co-terminal terms, qualified shared curve and origin cash, explicit shift and loading inputs, exact-grid terminal-measure simulation and cash, training-only fit and held-out price, and paired selected-model curve risk. Reconcile the existing pricer, policy, scalar calibration helper, risk, reports and hosts. Qualify support, cut, finite-grid, stopping and replay behavior independently. [Accepted record 09](valuations-rates/09-lmm-bermudans.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-lmm-bermudan-pricing-and-risk).

- **Reconciliation — Accepted Hull–White swaption exercise, tree and LSMC risk.** Record 08 was accepted on 2026-11-14 for exact single-curve co-terminal deliverables, qualified settlement, exact checked lattice and shared exercise diagnostics, joint Gaussian discounting and held-out centered-state policy pricing, and explicit curve/sigma risk and paired stochastic repricing. Reconcile the existing pricers, active sources, calibration and prepared state, reports and hosts. Qualify complete exercise, discounting, policy and risk routes. [Accepted record 08](valuations-rates/08-hull-white-swaptions.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-hull-white-swaption-exercise-and-risk).

- **Observed — Quoted futures risk can fail after clearing a usable quote.** Model eligibility accepts any adjustment, any surface ID or every non-Simple compounding mode without confirming inputs. DV01 clears the live quote and propagates missing-HW/wrong-or-missing-surface errors. Reproduce the result for quoted overnight cases with inactive model inputs. [Record 10](valuations-rates/10-rate-futures.md).

- **Reconciliation — Accepted rate futures reference, convexity, clearing and option rate risk.** Record 10 was accepted on 2026-11-14 for resolved contract and publication authority, weighted benchmark projection, explicit final-rate adjustment or qualified Hull–White moments, observed/model/final mark selection, single cash ownership, and separate mapped option and dated discount risk. Reconcile pricers, calibration materialization, reports and hosts. Qualify venue and settlement boundaries, partial blocks, model moments and distinct risk bases. [Accepted record 10](valuations-rates/10-rate-futures.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-rate-futures-and-futures-option-risk).

- **Observed — Inflation price, inverse and reporting domains differ.** Price and flow paths can accept nonpositive ending CPI even though zero-coupon par inversion rejects a nonpositive ratio. YoY raw flows include valuation-date payments that PV excludes. Apply the accepted endpoint validation and payment cut. [Record 13](valuations-rates/13-inflation-swaps.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-inflation-swap-cashflows-and-risk).

- **Observed — CMS risk and cashflow export can mix methods.** Convexity risk subtracts a zero-correction Black value from a selected replication base; projected cashflows use the approximation. Default Vanna differentiates only local Black plus convexity-vega terms, omitting other smile/forward effects. Adopt method-consistent labels and independent full-derivative evidence. Records: [11-CMS-CONVEXITY](valuations-rates/11-cms-convexity.md), [12-CMS-REPLICATION](valuations-rates/12-cms-replication.md).

- **Observed — Repo collateral ratios discard currency.** Coverage and annualized static excess discard the currency of both amounts. The accepted package requires dated dirty quote units in the cash currency and retires the annualized excess measure. [Accepted record 15](valuations-rates/15-repo.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-repo-cashflows-collateral-and-risk).

## Bond valuations

[Scope inventory](valuations-bonds/README.md).

- **Investigate — Merton step-up and custom coupon economics.** The scalar-coupon engine reads only the first step-up rate and extracts custom dates/accruals while leaving authored custom cash unused. Reproduce no-default schedule PV and qualify the accepted exact generated-parent program and rejection of supplied custom schedules. [Accepted record 08](valuations-bonds/08-merton-simulation.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-structural-bond-simulation-and-outputs).

- **Investigate — Merton calibration and final discount basis differ.** Calibration precedes dated factor preparation and reports the trial residual. Reproduce curved-term targets and qualify the accepted single dated basis across trial, independent cash validation and contractual pricing, with distinct diagnostics. [Accepted record 09](valuations-bonds/09-merton-calibration.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-cash-equivalent-structural-calibration).

- **Investigate — Merton settlement target misses carry terms.** The dirty target uses a security factor without the authorized matched buyer claim basis; YTM and other target types can use different entitlement. Reproduce coupon and ex-coupon events across settlement and qualify the accepted financed buyer-claim objective, including candidate-dependent claim differences. [Accepted record 09](valuations-bonds/09-merton-calibration.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-cash-equivalent-structural-calibration).

- **Investigate — Dynamic joint floating coupons may bypass ex-coupon entitlement.** Replay builds dynamic groups from raw metadata while static cash uses the holder-entitlement filter; no corresponding dynamic exclusion was found. Compare zero/positive-vol FRNs at ex-coupon boundaries and qualify the accepted original-parent entitlement gate across cash, PIK and retained claims. [Accepted record 06](valuations-bonds/06-joint-coupon-replay.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-joint-bond-payoff-and-recovery-replay).

- **Investigate — Joint and hazard recovery balances use different dates.** Joint replay applies principal-effective events; hazard/JTD retain cash repayment exposure through actual cash dates. Reproduce lagged and early principal settlement and compare separate economic principal and recoverable-par views, including their default-risk use. [Accepted record 06](valuations-bonds/06-joint-coupon-replay.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-joint-bond-payoff-and-recovery-replay) and [default risk](valuations-bonds/17-default-risk.md).

- **Reconciliation — Accepted bond asset-swap structures and quote basis.** Record 13 was accepted on 2026-11-14. Reconcile the price-aware par and dirty-notional proceeds laws, exact settlement entitlement and principal match, explicit dated floating and source roles, checked inverses, routing and published meanings. Qualify settlement, market convention, runtime and host surfaces independently. [Accepted record 13](valuations-bonds/13-asset-swaps.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-bond-asset-swaps-and-price-inversion).

- **Observed — CS01 comment confuses absolute and relative finite-difference error.** Source prose claims forward/central agreement around 1e-8 of CS01 magnitude; the usual expansion gives O(h²) absolute price difference and O(h) relative first-order sensitivity. Correct the claim and specify measured tolerance; this alone is not a pricing defect. Records: [16](valuations-bonds/16-spread-credit-risk.md).

[Scope inventory](valuations-other-fixed-income/README.md).

- **Observed — Convertible model identity differs from residual terminology.** Total and cash components share DF*survival continuation plus fractional recovery; surviving stock drift includes hazard. This is not split-discount Tsiveriotis–Fernandes. Reconcile model/risk names and qualify the intended credit formulation independently. Records: [02](valuations-other-fixed-income/02-convertible-lattice.md), [03](valuations-other-fixed-income/03-convertible-quotes-risk.md).

- **Reconciliation — Convertible settlement quotes, inverse fits and risk.** Implement the accepted settlement buyer-claim bridge, cash accrued, comparable diagnostics, separate OAS and implied hazard-spread fits, held-basis risk, and explicit spot ladder. Qualify settlement, quote and credit replay fixtures, grid/bump stability and consumer agreement. [Accepted record 03](valuations-other-fixed-income/03-convertible-quotes-risk.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-convertible-quotes-and-risk).

- **Reconciliation — Inflation-bond reference, floors and nominal cash.** Implement the issue-specific reference and rounding, explicit point-in-time anchors and authorized release contingencies, qualified floors, real-par retirement and nominal PV/export. - **Observed — Revolver deterministic limits and current counterfactuals.** Tiny utilization sigma freezes drift and spread response, and the current Constant spread path uses a diffusing CIR. Record 11 accepts deterministic reversion and literal Constant behavior. Reconcile dependent expected-loss and draw-option definitions in the metric owner. [Accepted record 11](valuations-other-fixed-income/11-revolver-monte-carlo-credit.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-revolver-simulation-and-default-valuation), [record 12](valuations-other-fixed-income/12-revolver-quotes-risk.md).

- **Reconciliation — Revolver simulation, survival and default valuation.** Implement the accepted named routes, phased joint funding, fitted-bank discount, explicit credit authority and one-horizon CIR mean fit, claim-level survival, phased prompt default, fixed-budget Philox estimates and qualified expected cash and capture. Qualify empirical and market inputs, independent survival and limiting prices, grid convergence and host results. [Accepted record 11](valuations-other-fixed-income/11-revolver-monte-carlo-credit.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-revolver-simulation-and-default-valuation).

- **Observed — Revolver expected-loss rerun and risk dispatch.** Current expected loss can skip active spread models without a hazard ID, and removing spread can change utilization despite replaying the seed. Current registered credit metrics can select risk from curve presence. The accepted contract binds expected loss to the same paths and selects risk by experiment. [Accepted record 12](valuations-other-fixed-income/12-revolver-quotes-risk.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-revolver-quotes-exposure-and-risk).

Qualify servicing, assignment, replay, paired risk, and host results. [Accepted record 12](valuations-other-fixed-income/12-revolver-quotes-risk.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-revolver-quotes-exposure-and-risk).

[Accepted record 15](valuations-other-fixed-income/15-tba-and-dollar-roll.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-tba-settlement-and-dollar-rolls).

[Accepted COMMODITY-SWAPTION](valuations-fx-and-exotics/commodity-swaption.md#accepted-decision-for-commodity-swaption-pricing-and-exercise).

- **Reconciliation — Commodity spread Kirk, fixing and active leg risk.** Implement exact normalized leg sources, checked named Kirk and zero-variance limits, authorized pair and dated cash, explicit leg deltas, shared-grid vega and corresponding host reports. Independently qualify approximation error across the signed strike and correlation domain, source and smile behavior, settlement and risk. [Accepted COMMODITY-SPREAD](valuations-fx-and-exotics/commodity-spread.md#accepted-decision-for-commodity-spread-pricing-and-settlement).

## Structured credit

[Scope inventory](valuations-structured-credit/README.md).

- **Observed — Ordinary stochastic dispatch discards richer dependence presets.** Scenario construction starts SingleFactor/kappa zero, copies scalar asset correlation and changes mean reversion only for intensity. Preset prepay/default -0.30/-0.20 does not select private TwoFactor support, and Matrix/Sectored dependence collapses to scalar. Reconcile effective factors and correlation and identify deterministic discounting in provenance. [Accepted record 15](valuations-structured-credit/15-stochastic-waterfall.md#accepted-decision-for-stochastic-waterfall-pricing-and-estimation).

- **Reconciliation — Structured-credit OAS scenario and settlement fit.** Implement explicit rate and credit composition, matched dated projections, original-origin buyer discounting and settlement entitlement, fixed sampling, a monotone checked spread inverse and native-currency residuals. Qualify non-base-date and future-settlement paths, rate/credit calibration, deterministic reduction, convergence and existing host paths. [Accepted record 17](valuations-structured-credit/17-oas-scenarios.md#accepted-decision-for-structured-credit-oas-and-settlement-spread-fitting).

- **Reconciliation — Principal regimes and dated early amortization.** Implement named dated predicates, separate scheduled and unscheduled allocation, targeted OC, controlled accumulation and composed mandatory and optional rules. Qualify calendar-month history, reversible protections, latched acceleration and existing host paths. [Accepted record 13](valuations-structured-credit/13-principal-regimes-and-amortization.md#accepted-decision-for-principal-regimes-and-early-amortization).

- **Reconciliation — Statistical collateral timing, cash and floating rates.** Implement actual monthly survival, strict dated target amounts, default/scheduled/prepayment order, contractual installment attrition, uniform fixing and floor rules, and default-vintage recoveries. Qualify broken periods, term resets, heterogeneous collateral timing, recovery queues and shared host and model routes. [Accepted record 03](valuations-structured-credit/03-statistical-collateral-projection.md#accepted-decision-for-statistical-collateral-projection).

- **Observed — Coupon claims and accrued metadata use different loss balances.** Actual claims use balance after current realized-loss writedown; quote accrued retains pre-writedown opening balance. Reconcile claims to the accepted entitlement phase and resolve quote accrued under its own basis. [Accepted record 11](valuations-structured-credit/11-waterfall-claims-and-rounding.md#accepted-decision-for-waterfall-claims-and-rounding) · [Quote record 20](valuations-structured-credit/20-quotes-and-inverse-pricing.md#accepted-decision-for-structured-credit-settlement-quotes-and-inversion).

- **Reconciliation — Loss recognition, calls and terminal cash.** Implement explicit loss and note-balance elections, capped incremental allocation, funded calls, retained or assigned claims, authorized terminal recipients and legal discharge. Remove family defaults and the absolute exhaustion threshold. Qualify seasoned state, transaction terms, call shortfalls, recovery tails, residual and incentive allocation, ledger conservation and existing host paths. [Accepted record 14](valuations-structured-credit/14-loss-recognition-and-terminal-cash.md#accepted-decision-for-loss-recognition-and-terminal-cash).

- **Reconciliation — Structured-credit dispatch and assumption authority.** Implement distinct deterministic and stochastic routes, authoritative collateral, explicit active inputs, versioned compatible starter profiles, separate fee selection and contractual schedule and discounting. Qualify profile and effective-role provenance, route-specific metrics, same-day cash, the resolved stochastic request and existing host paths. - **Evidence — Agency CMO deal benchmarks.** Obtain external deal and PAC benchmarks for the authorized agency pool, class and support rules, seasoned distributions and varying prepayment paths. Qualify endpoint-PSA schedule limits on actual deals. [Accepted record 18](valuations-structured-credit/18-agency-cmo-and-pac.md#accepted-decision-for-agency-cmo-waterfalls-and-dated-pac-contracts).

- **Reconciliation — Borrowing-base facilities and lender cash.** Implement dated eligibility and concentration composition, draw sequencing and cure assessments, actual fee sources and slices, qualified term-out, signed lender cash and explicit return and risk bases. Qualify filed facility benchmarks, schedules, fee cash, borrower and lender reconciliation and existing host paths. - **Reconciliation — Structured-credit risk, breakeven and scenario grids.** Implement the fixed-yield and live rate bases, checked Z and par-credit risk, active collateral coordinates and full valid stencils, typed breakeven CDR and deterministic grids with affected exposures. Qualify nonlinear reprices, loss monotonicity, scenario boundaries, matched stochastic comparisons and existing hosts. [Accepted record 11](valuations-structured-credit/11-waterfall-claims-and-rounding.md#accepted-decision-for-waterfall-claims-and-rounding).

- **Reconciliation — Coverage numerators and cure diversion.** Implement dated eligible balances, noncumulative adjustments, prepared IC claims, breach state and authorized executable cures. ## Calibration: quotes and curve/smile targets

[Scope inventory](calibration-targets/README.md).

- **Reconciliation — Bind discount calibration to the fitted output role.** Remove the separate pricing discount ID and use the exact candidate for each trial, probe and final reprice. Preserve independent projection dependencies and apply only qualified shared overnight links. [Accepted record 003](calibration-targets/03-discount-calibration.md#accepted-decision-for-explicit-discount-calibration-and-delivered-curve-fit).

- **Reconciliation — Explicit single-name CDS hazard calibration and par-risk qualification.** Reconcile exact live CDS preparation and recovery, signed buyer residuals, explicit bounded intensity fitting, delivered-market repricing and paired original and model-implied par observations. Remove the hidden source cap and qualify trial bounds independently of elected economic checks. Qualify distressed and near-zero-LGD fits, irregular maturities, maturity versus settlement and valuation cut, zero-par spread eligibility, solver conditioning, dependency and recovery replay, restoration and hosts. [Accepted record 005](calibration-targets/05-hazard-calibration.md#accepted-decision-for-explicit-single-name-hazard-calibration-and-par-risk-qualification).

- **Reconciliation — Explicit CPI source anchors, live swap fitting and publication.** Separate the source origin from the contractual starting CPI, freeze fixing vintages and release facts, and resolve actual projected support for each eligible swap. Reconcile explicit positive bounds and seeds, prefix-qualified bootstrap and identified simultaneous fits, and delivered native repricing. Remove the inverted clamp path and qualify extreme rebasing, nonlinear interpolation, mixed ZC and YoY identification, contractual reference months and scheduled releases, observed and projected reference continuity, restoration and hosts. [Accepted record 006](calibration-targets/06-inflation-calibration.md#accepted-decision-for-explicit-cpi-anchors-and-delivered-inflation-calibration).

- **Reconciliation — Consistent foreign-factor construction and delivered fit.** Remove the hard factor cap and use the same positive-factor construction, explicit source clock, interpolation and tails for trials and delivery. Keep the valuation cut separate from the curve origin. Qualify negative rates, actual dated pricing support, native residual gates, elected economic checks, atomic publication and restoration. [Accepted record 007](calibration-targets/07-xccy-calibration.md#accepted-decision-for-collateral-qualified-foreign-discount-calibration).

- **Reconciliation — Collateral-qualified cross-currency quote selection and fit.** Reconcile eligible XCCY and mixed quote sets, explicit collateral, sizing and valuation FX authorities, the actual foreign support pillar, prefix-qualified bootstrap and identified bounded simultaneous fitting. Qualify independent pair-specific spot and basis orientation, pending and published FX resets, benchmark instruments and dated market data. Preserve lossless construction evidence for separately qualified quote replay. Link diagnostic removal and reporting to the [dated zero-rate diagnostic contract](core-market/basis-spreads.md#accepted-decision-source-derived-dated-zero-rate-differences). [Accepted record 007](calibration-targets/07-xccy-calibration.md#accepted-decision-for-collateral-qualified-foreign-discount-calibration).

- **Reconciliation — Exact dated swaption references and calibration forwards.** Reuse the checked IRS coupon and European swaption preparation with explicit curve and fixing roles, native volatility clock, canonical nominal tenor, and eligible cash on actual payment dates. Qualify required published fixings, overnight observation transforms, swap and payment lags, tenor collisions, permitted spread mappings and exact references for synthetic targets. Retire rounded-month and index-default reconstruction. Retain source and construction provenance through restoration and existing hosts. [Accepted record 013](calibration-targets/13-swaption-forwards.md#accepted-decision-for-dated-swaption-coordinates-and-calibration-forwards).

- **Reconciliation — Explicit rate preparation, native residuals and objective weights.** Implement frozen prepared contracts and clocks, distinct quote and knot identity, isolated role binding, explicit cash scales and native residual definitions, mixed-unit weights and exact final repricing. Qualify signed rates, contractual cash rounding, near or collapsed coordinates, objective scales, zero-weight fit gates and restoration. Target-specific grids, bounds and weighting outside the shared rate fit retain their owners. For discount calibration reconcile dependency-qualified standalone bootstrap with explicit factor-space search and root evidence, bounded simultaneous zeros, elected seeds and correctly labeled weighted derivatives. For projection calibration reconcile the independently declared grid, exact index and discount bindings, live native quote residuals, identified bounded simultaneous factor fitting and exact output replay. - **Reconciliation — Exact equity smile quote, spot and dated carry inputs.** Reconcile explicit live quote selection, exact spot authority, carry and funding bindings, actual fixing phases and dividend entitlement with payment-date discounting. Retire implicit spot, yield, schedule and SVI zero-rate defaults. Remove rounded grouping, geometric floors, parameter clipping and corner substitutions. Qualify genuine fitted support, exact node references, near-zero nu, both evaluation modes, native delivered replay, omitted interior smiles, atomic admission, restoration and existing hosts. [Accepted record 014](calibration-targets/14-swaption-cube.md#accepted-decision-for-exact-sabr-swaption-smiles-and-strict-cube-publication).

Remove fuzzy maturity and detachment matching, approximate knots, the fixed native budget and synthetic anchors. Other inspected tests mainly use hand formulas, same-kernel quotes, round trips and invariants. Obtain independent dated curves, surfaces and credit benchmarks for the selected target formulations. For equity SABR, qualify sparse and extreme strikes, literal beta endpoints on the unshifted Black domain, tiny positive quoted vol, exact ATM branches, same-strike companion checks, multistart reproducibility, source calendar ordering and delivered-grid accuracy. For equity SVI, qualify distinct-strike support, flat and nonlinear slices, native errors, finite scan coverage, fitted tails and independent delivered-grid replay. For swaption forwards, qualify independent dated term and overnight market fixtures, including publication cut, observation transforms, delayed payment, spread mapping and cross-calendar parity. For the swaption SABR cube, qualify independent native-law market smiles, exact and interpolated nodes, sparse support, signed shifts and delivered-all-quote replay. For base correlation, qualify independent tranche prices, loss quadrature, local root sensitivity, attachment support and both ordering policies on the delivered curve. For single-tranche Student-t fitting, qualify independent dated tranche benchmarks, root sensitivity relative to pricing allowances, and final fit on the exact published df. For rate and hazard replay, qualify independent prepared quote bindings, full evaluator reproduction, native action repricing, transformation invalidation and restoration. Under ADR 0032, review independent inputs, provenance and tolerances for linked Hagan–West formula, failed-fit and attributed Bloomberg OIS cases, then run the relevant scoped suites.
