## Calibration: Hull–White and LMM

[Scope inventory](calibration-rate-models/README.md).

- **Observed — HW control descriptions have inconsistent units.** The vega floor is numerically 1e-8 but described as one basis point of annuity-year; restart half-width 0.5 is additive in log parameters, implying natural factors about 0.61–1.65 rather than literal ±50%. Reconcile the source descriptions with the implemented controls. Records: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-006](calibration-rate-models/06-two-parameter-fit.md).

- **Observed — Cap vega diagnostic prose differs from calculation.** Market vega is evaluated at each quote's actual strike, although metadata commentary calls it ATM. Clarify the meaning wherever this diagnostic is exposed. Record: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-006](calibration-rate-models/06-two-parameter-fit.md).

- **Observed — HW construction is not a numerical arbitrage certificate.** Swaption source prose says model construction eliminates the need for output arbitrage checks; actual gates inspect individual prices, roots and residuals. Narrow the guarantee and distinguish mathematical-model assumptions from the computed surface and synthetic decomposition. No arbitrage failure was reproduced. Records: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-004](calibration-rate-models/04-jamshidian-decomposition.md).

- **Reconciliation — Bounded fixed-κ cap and floor premium fitting.** Reconcile equal-weight unit-notional premiums, qualified sigma branch selection and checked golden-section work. Retire inherited vega admission, the unused seed, hardcoded bounds, relative moneyness and the fixed iteration label. Qualify delayed-payment branches, low-volatility and endpoint identification, complete native fit gates and evidence, restoration and host paths. [Accepted record 007](calibration-rate-models/07-fixed-kappa-cap-fit.md#accepted-decision-for-bounded-fixed-kappa-cap-and-floor-premium-fitting).

- **Reconciliation — Bounded identified two-parameter Hull–White fitting.** Reconcile dimensionless tolerance-scaled premium errors, checked open parameter maps, augmented QR, elected Halton starts and local identification. Apply native fit and output gates before ranking, preserve exact quote IDs and distinct objective and native evidence, and qualify restored and host paths. [Accepted record 006](calibration-rate-models/06-two-parameter-fit.md#accepted-decision-for-bounded-identified-two-parameter-hull-white-fitting).

- **Reconciliation — Exact Hull–White ATM swaption targets and native quote reconstruction.** Reconcile dated par and benchmark roles, the exact synthetic grid, independent quote clocks, the analytic inverse and checked premium agreement on the returned volatility. Retire the small-annuity and vega fallbacks. Qualify exercise/fixing chronology, irregular dates, both Black premium boundaries, and native fit and restoration across hosts. [Accepted record 003](calibration-rate-models/03-atm-swaption-quotes.md#accepted-decision-for-exact-atm-swaption-targets-and-native-quote-reconstruction).

- **Evidence — Qualify local identification on independent Hull–White baskets.** Compare the accepted sensitivity gate with noisy and repeated-economic quotes, mixed laws, parameter stability, explicit bounds and restart coverage. Quantify pricing resolution and derivative uncertainty on the fitted objective scale. [Accepted record 006](calibration-rate-models/06-two-parameter-fit.md#accepted-decision-for-bounded-identified-two-parameter-hull-white-fitting).

- **Observed — Final HW reports retain optimizer-scale metadata.** Main residuals are replaced with exact reconstructed quote-vol errors, but inherited final_* optimizer statistics still describe vega-linearized residuals. Preserve that distinction in field interpretation and inspect consumers before treating mixed-scale metadata as a numerical defect. Records: [CALIBRATION-RATE-MODELS-006](calibration-rate-models/06-two-parameter-fit.md).

- **Reconciliation — Bounded live-fixing Hull–White sigma bootstrap.** Reconcile exact strip identity and fixing support, explicit per-segment branch controls, symmetric endpoint admission, local response and native quote gates. Reprice and re-invert on the final schedule, report actual work, and coordinate exact dated or numeric state, atomic publication and schema wording with the adapter. Qualify both delayed-payment branches, noisy or adjacent pillars, bound sensitivity, market references, restoration and host paths. [Accepted record 008](calibration-rate-models/08-piecewise-sigma-bootstrap.md#accepted-decision-for-live-fixing-hullwhite-sigma-bootstrapping).

- **Reconciliation — Single-slice LMM loading-scale calibration.** Share product-owned contractual accruals, original period indices, explicit displacement and two-lane profile. Reconcile strict native Black query and clocks, checked analytic ATM conversion, returned-scale replay and native acceptance, exact fit evidence and explicit host and pricing consumption. Correct the Rust reference to the accepted 2002 sections and qualify shifted premium caps and basis restoration. [Accepted record 009](calibration-rate-models/09-lmm-rebonato-calibration.md#accepted-decision-for-single-slice-lmm-loading-scale-calibration).

- **Reconciliation — Checked synthetic Jamshidian decomposition.** Reconcile the exact regular signed coupon profile, standardized exercise state, explicit Brent bracket, PV root allowance and checked weighted bond puts. Retire Newton and heuristic fallback brackets, small-variance shortcuts and price clipping. Qualify both coupon signs, loading differences near saturation, strike cancellation, deterministic limits, independent premium references and final fit behavior. [Accepted record 004](calibration-rate-models/04-jamshidian-decomposition.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-synthetic-jamshidian-decomposition).

- **Reconciliation — Checked contractual Gaussian swaption integration.** Reconcile the selected dated coupon laws, payment covariance, checked signed payoff, explicit tail and quadrature allowances and whole-panel missed-exercise checks. Preserve the distinction between analytic bounds, the operational estimator and native quote acceptance. Qualify multiple exercise regions, long horizons, near-zero premiums, pricing resolution at low vega, independent references and restored host behavior. [Accepted record 005](calibration-rate-models/05-contractual-gaussian-integration.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-contractual-gaussian-swaption-integration).

- **Reconciliation — Checked Hull–White cap and floor strips and implied Normal inversion.** Reconcile exact live and synthetic schedules, benchmark accrual, separate clocks, actual payment-measure prices, bounded strip inversion and paired gross and time-value acceptance. Remove automatic bracket expansion and blanket vega admission. Qualify irregular periods, signed rates, low vega, early and delayed payment, near-intrinsic pricing, independent reference provenance and restoration across hosts. [Accepted record 002](calibration-rate-models/02-cap-schedules-and-quote-inversion.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-cap-and-floor-strips-and-implied-normal-inversion).

- **Evidence — Quantify the multi-forward LMM approximation.** Frozen-formula round trips and the independent one-forward premium case leave multi-forward error unmeasured. Compare the accepted single-target European approximation with independently converged multi-forward European prices and qualify dated market and host fit consumption. The Bermudan pricing scope owns separate exercise and simulation qualification. [Accepted record 009](calibration-rate-models/09-lmm-rebonato-calibration.md#accepted-decision-for-single-slice-lmm-loading-scale-calibration).

- **Reconciliation — Explicit Hull–White calibration entry and quote domains.** Reconcile explicit dated and synthetic profiles, exact curve roles and clocks, typed quote laws, elected fit controls and native repricing. Preserve complete fitted state and original knot support. Qualify irregular calendars, independent curve adaptation, payment lags, mixed quote units, fit identification, restore and host behavior. [Accepted record 001](calibration-rate-models/01-curve-entry-and-quote-domains.md#accepted-decision-for-explicit-hull-white-calibration-entry-and-quote-domains).

## Statements: capital structure

[Scope inventory](statements-capital/README.md).

- **Reconciliation — Opening principal must match between the schedule and debt ledger.** Replace raw opening-balance scaling for carried debt with a hard mismatch error naming the instrument, period, currency and both opening amounts. Calculate debt service from the dated schedule after checking principal at the same economic boundary. Qualify dated issuance, redraw and retirement cases. [Accepted opening principal decision](statements-capital/03-funding-and-balances.md#accepted-decision-for-opening-principal-reconciliation).

- **Investigate — New-funding closes mix economic and cash-period clocks.** A zero-opening/new-funding close combines economic-dated funding with cash-period PIK and repayments, whereas ordinary close uses economic outstanding. Reproduce newly issued or redrawn debt with early/late settlement and PIK dates to measure closing-balance and capacity differences. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-003](statements-capital/03-funding-and-balances.md).

- **Investigate — Class allocation can direct floating remainder to an ineligible final row.** Class vectors contain zeros for other rows, but pro-rata remainder goes to the final array entry, potentially zero/ineligible, before available cash becomes zero. Independently bound or reproduce the residual across scales and row orders before concluding a material cash loss; ordinary conservation fixtures do not cover every case. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-006](statements-capital/06-priority-allocation.md).

- **Evidence — Residual coupons need independent debt-ledger reconciliation.** Qualify arbitrary cash/PIK shares, zeroed-coupon reactivation, irregular day counts and multiple dated advances/arrears against an independent schedule. Existing residual regressions primarily establish internal ledger invariants. Include tolerance scaling while preserving the established period-close balance-change and FX/date policy. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-005](statements-capital/05-residual-coupons.md).

- **Reconciliation — Explicit bond and swap construction.** Remove the four Statements construction helpers across Rust, Python and WASM and reconcile callers to the canonical Valuations instrument and the existing `add_debt` or `addDebt` route. Verify that resolved contractual terms, source and schedule survive model attachment and replay. Independently qualify the supported instrument schedules. [Accepted construction decision](statements-capital/01-instrument-construction.md#accepted-decision-for-explicit-bond-and-swap-construction).

- **Reconciliation — Debt and hedge cashflow classification and period admission.** Validate periods before reading schedules, enforce source perspective and native currency, and classify dated cash, principal and PIK effects, fees and hedge settlements under the accepted contract. Keep signed distress cash visible outside scheduled debt service. Period coupon netting, separate expense and income totals and the Interest claim are implemented. Independently qualify source conversion, custom and rebuilt schedules, signed fee and distress cash, explicit liability changes and boundary dates against borrower and hedge ledgers. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-002](statements-capital/02-cashflow-classification.md#accepted-decision-for-period-admission-and-cashflow-classification).

- **Reconciliation — Contractual accrued cash interest must stay separate from arrears.** Calculate future-due cash interest earned through period close from canonical earning terms, dated principal and the required accrual context. Replace generic accrual inputs and report overdue arrears separately. Qualify period-close earning and due-date boundaries. Issue-discount amortization and effective-interest accounting remain separate from contractual accrual and the funding face ledger. [Accepted contractual accrual decision](statements-capital/03-funding-and-balances.md#accepted-decision-for-contractual-accrued-cash-interest).

- **Reconciliation — Dated principal claims, settlement arrears and advance reserves.** Require stable dated claims with separate cash and face, remove synthetic claims, and report economic principal, unsettled payments, arrears and paid advance reserves separately. Reconcile scheduled principal across seniority classes, eligible instrument claims and due dates. Qualify partial, early and late payments and exact native-currency balances with atomic state updates across periods. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-004](statements-capital/04-dated-principal.md#accepted-decision-for-dated-principal-claims).

- **Reconciliation — Canonical residual coupon rebuilding.** Rebuild affected original coupon parents, remaining principal and balance-sensitive fees from authorized dated events and canonical product terms. Preserve settled cash, earned interest, locked observations, cash and PIK dates, and dated claim identity. Commit the schedule and debt ledger together after validation. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-005](statements-capital/05-residual-coupons.md#accepted-decision-for-residual-coupon-rebuilding).

- **Reconciliation — Exact waterfall cash allocation and conservation.** Enforce transitive same-period cash-pool independence, category and seniority rules, exact or authorized-quantum allocation within caps, preservation of every contractual shortfall and terminal Equity or retained cash. Qualify debt-free and negative pools, subcent balances, stable remainder allocation and exact conservation with atomic schedule, finance state and totals. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-006](statements-capital/06-priority-allocation.md#accepted-decision-for-waterfall-cash-allocation-and-conservation).

- **Reconciliation — Agreement-defined prepayments and excess-cash-flow sweeps.** Implement independently elected at-par priorities, explicit eligibility, dated agreement-specific sizing and deductions, principal and cash caps, and requested, paid and unpaid reporting. Qualify mixed term-loan and revolver allocation, supported payoff terms, signed inputs and dated arrears with independent agreement examples. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-007](statements-capital/07-prepayments-and-ecf.md#accepted-decision-for-agreement-defined-prepayments-and-ecf-sweeps).

- **Reconciliation — Contractual PIK elections and carried claims.** Implement authorized dated coupon elections, finite matching-unit thresholds, coupon-period duration, elected rate and split, and dated capitalization with separate arrears. Qualify amendments, full payoff, typed and wire validation, and atomic election, ledger and schedule replay across retries and reporting cadences. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-008](statements-capital/08-pik-and-carry-state.md#accepted-decision-for-contractual-pik-elections-and-carried-claims).

- **Reconciliation — Capital reporting, period-close FX and atomic replacement.** Implement distinct currency scopes, explicit inclusive `PeriodEnd` FX with provenance and unavailable totals on missing quotes, complete component reconciliation with known-zero semantics, exact currency-tagged decimal exports and chronological reads. Replace whole periods atomically and reject reporting-currency conflicts. Qualify native and consolidated results independently across dated FX changes and stale-row replacement. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-009](statements-capital/09-reporting-and-fx.md#accepted-decision-for-capital-reporting-and-fx).

- **Evidence — Capital and waterfall policy lacks independent contractual reconciliation.** Selected tests cover formulas, boundaries, routing, state and internal conservation, but no independent debt ledger, lender ECF definition, legal waterfall, accounting-standard or currency/FX qualification was established. Obtain evidence scoped to the adopted contracts rather than treating test names as compliance. Records: [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-004](statements-capital/04-dated-principal.md), [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-006](statements-capital/06-priority-allocation.md), [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-007](statements-capital/07-prepayments-and-ecf.md), [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-008](statements-capital/08-pik-and-carry-state.md), [STATEMENTS-CAPITAL-009](statements-capital/09-reporting-and-fx.md).

## Margin: collateral and initial margin

[Scope inventory](margin-collateral-im/README.md).

- **Reconciliation — Complete SIMM parameters and checked dependence.** Require canonical profiles with positive denominators, an immutable reference horizon and every consumed pair. Apply the read-only core gate with the accepted normalized pivot tolerance. Qualify effective currency, issuer and concentration-adjusted dependence, signed coefficients, typed variance failures and host restoration. Cover missing pairs, zero thresholds and indefinite in-range coefficients. [Accepted SIMM parameter contract](margin-collateral-im/02-simm-parameter-validation.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-simm-parameter-profiles-and-correlation-validation).

- **Reconciliation — One checked immutable margin parameter snapshot.** Require an elected bundled or complete custom snapshot. Make schema and version tags required and reject duplicate interpreted keys. Reconcile loader, direct constructor, consumer and restored validation, including schedule rate bounds and model horizon checks. Carry selected profile identity through callers and hosts, cache the bundled result or error, and remove recursive overlay and fallback paths. Field-specific rules remain with [collateral](margin-collateral-im/15-collateral-eligibility-and-haircuts.md) and [CSA/IM terms](margin-collateral-im/17-csa-selection-and-im-terms.md). [Accepted registry contract](margin-collateral-im/01-registry-and-defaults.md#accepted-decision-for-explicit-margin-parameter-snapshots-and-contractual-terms).

- **Reconciliation — Explicit dated margin terms and calculation routes.** Replace inferred amounts, roles and clocks with actual allocated currency-bearing terms and explicit method, collateral and funding inputs. Preserve the selected source when changing agreement terms. Qualify missing versus explicit zero or no IM, scoped external CCP versus proxy routing, and time metadata versus implemented behavior. Calculation and lifecycle rules remain with [CSA/IM terms](margin-collateral-im/17-csa-selection-and-im-terms.md) and the route owners. [Accepted contractual terms](margin-collateral-im/01-registry-and-defaults.md#accepted-decision-for-explicit-margin-parameter-snapshots-and-contractual-terms).

- **Observed — Collateral concentration is checked per row rather than per class.** Each allocation row is compared with the first matching class limit, without grouping or finite/nonnegative validation. Two 20% rows can each pass a 30% limit while their class total is 40%; this is a source-derived example, not an executed test. Define aggregation and input-domain requirements. Define negative, zero, NaN and mixed-unit input behavior, automatic-enforcement ownership and issuer-level scope. Add an independent contractual reference for the intended concentration covenant. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-016](margin-collateral-im/16-collateral-concentration.md).

- **Observed — Haircut helper and strict calculator select different eligibility rules.** Lightweight lookup/is_eligible ignores some metadata and permits default_haircut, while the strict calculator enforces maturity/rating and ignores that default. Base plus FX haircut is uncapped, overlap uses first-match inclusive tolerance, and the Treasury schedule does not identify issuer/country. Agree helper scope, boundary precedence and total-haircut domain. Include missing-metadata behavior, ordered maturity-range/default-limit validation across construction paths, and independent qualification of every schedule cell and actual security eligibility. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-015](margin-collateral-im/15-collateral-eligibility-and-haircuts.md).

- **Observed — External CCP inputs lack amount and MPOR validation.** External IM and direct/provided MPOR are not checked for nonnegative amount, expected currency or positive horizon. Define the external-source contract and reproduce invalid values at public calculator boundaries. Specify source-date provenance and qualify the empirical meaning of the proxy being conservative; no bound or CCP-reference accuracy has been established. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-014](margin-collateral-im/14-ccp-external-and-proxy.md).

- **Observed — CCP fallback retains an external horizon override.** An external MPOR override persists even when the external IM amount is absent and the proxy supplies the result. Agree whether proxy/external metadata may mix and add a targeted source-fallback fixture. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-014](margin-collateral-im/14-ccp-external-and-proxy.md).

- **Observed — ICE name resolution selects different clearing houses.** for_ccp("ICE") selects ClearUS, while the registry alias ice selects ClearCredit. Reconcile identity/default routing before applying clearing-house-specific parameters. Also document and adopt unknown-name routing explicitly. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-014](margin-collateral-im/14-ccp-external-and-proxy.md).

- **Observed — Margin settlement-day reporting uses calendar days.** MarginCall::days_to_settle subtracts dates as elapsed calendar days despite business-day Rustdoc. Agree the intended displayed metric and reconcile the documentation or implementation with calendar-aware evidence. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-019](margin-collateral-im/19-margin-dates-and-replay.md).

- **Investigate — Repo excess returns can exceed previously posted cash.** After the first observation, excess returns are not capped by prior cash margin, so the projected margin balance can become negative. Reproduce declining exposure/collateral paths and establish whether negative balances represent an intended receivable or an unsupported return. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-020](margin-collateral-im/20-repo-margin-cashflows.md).

- **Decision — Repo margin projection has a narrow event contract.** All active repo types share one ratio algorithm; call_frequency and substitution fields do not drive projection, and no initial excess return or terminal unwind is synthesized. Interest uses caller-provided balances and a fixed 0.01-major-unit filter. Agree chronology, parameter domains, currency precision and omitted-event treatment. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-020](margin-collateral-im/20-repo-margin-cashflows.md).

- **Observed — Shared sensitivity-table transport has validation and context gaps.** Ordinary FRTB row import omits final container validation. Detached rows omit basis metadata and can discard populated unused columns. Resolve the FRTB admission rules, exact transport layout and metadata, and scope of external compatibility. Preserve the [accepted SIMM contribution and snapshot semantics](margin-collateral-im/03-sensitivity-inputs.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-simm-sensitivity-identity-scaling-and-netting) in the SIMM routes. Record: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-022](margin-collateral-im/22-wire-tables-and-provenance.md).

- **Reconciliation — Qualified, checked SIMM sensitivity state.** Require complete factor, scope, unit and FX basis. Remove scalar vector rebasing and unchecked mutation. Reconcile exact vertices, pair normalization, signed scaling, ordered accumulation, zero and expiry semantics, additive contribution rows and unique canonical snapshots through valuation, portfolio and host paths. Qualify mixed units and currencies, missing coordinates, same-currency pairs and failures before commit. [Accepted SIMM sensitivity contract](margin-collateral-im/03-sensitivity-inputs.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-simm-sensitivity-identity-scaling-and-netting).

- **Observed — Margin provenance descriptions overstate or lag the implementation.** Version and file-count corrections belong to the [central methodology-pins follow-up](../FOLLOW_UPS.md#methodology-pins). Schema prose asserts GMRA compliance, and an ImResult example marks SIMM non-approximate although the calculator marks it approximate. Reconcile those scope/status descriptions without replacing provenance with compliance claims. Correct the registry module’s broad decimal-rate units comment, and qualify both GMRA and ISDA schema prose against the actual scope. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-001](margin-collateral-im/01-registry-and-defaults.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-020](margin-collateral-im/20-repo-margin-cashflows.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-022](margin-collateral-im/22-wire-tables-and-provenance.md).

- **Reconciliation — Checked SIMM ordinary IR delta and vega.** Carry ordinary yield subcurve and vertex identity, exact currency groups and qualified vega source coordinates through adapter and model paths. Verify signed raw concentration, subcurve and expiry dependence, signed currency caps, effective correlation, range failures and separate reference-horizon charges through MVA, portfolio and hosts. [Accepted IR contract](margin-collateral-im/04-simm-interest-rate.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-simm-interest-rate-delta-and-vega-aggregation).

- **Reconciliation — Checked SIMM qualifying-credit delta and vega.** Carry exact issuer, seniority, payment currency and component vertex or qualified index volatility identity and dated bucket classification. Verify constituent delta, index vega, raw concentration, signed bucket caps, effective dependence and linear residual addition through the valuation, portfolio and host paths. [Accepted qualifying-credit contract](margin-collateral-im/05-simm-qualifying-credit.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-simm-qualifying-credit-delta-and-vega).

- **Reconciliation — Checked SIMM nonqualifying-credit delta and vega.** Supply all three buckets and the qualified tranche or index-volatility identity, separate classified correlation group and applicable spread vertex or option expiry. Verify raw concentration, signed caps, effective dependence and residual addition through valuation, portfolio, MVA and host paths. Track required curvature and assembly separately. [Accepted nonqualifying-credit contract](margin-collateral-im/06-simm-nonqualifying-credit.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-simm-nonqualifying-credit-delta-and-vega).

- **Reconciliation — Checked SIMM equity delta and vega.** Carry all 12 numbered buckets and explicit residual, dated classification, direct or constituent index treatment and the source-qualified volatility proxy. Verify signed concentration, historical and custom effective dependence, bucket 12 vega and linear residual addition through valuation, portfolio, MVA and host paths. [Accepted equity contract](margin-collateral-im/07-simm-equity.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-simm-equity-delta-and-vega).

- **Reconciliation — Checked SIMM FX delta and pair vega.** Supply qualified directed USD-basis delta and distinct canonical vega pairs with expiry and volatility evidence. Verify the two-group proxy table, three concentration categories, six pair thresholds, HVR order, signed high/high dependence and effective matrix failures through valuation, portfolio, MVA and host paths. [Accepted FX contract](margin-collateral-im/08-simm-fx.md#accepted-decision-for-checked-simm-fx-delta-and-pair-vega).

- **Decision — Approximate SIMM coverage and assembly.** Resolve commodity intra-bucket netting and concentration, and curvature expiry, squared correlations and residual conventions. Decide component and class assembly, product-class coverage, MPOR scaling outside ten days, approximation propagation and zero diagnostics. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-009](margin-collateral-im/09-simm-commodity.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-010](margin-collateral-im/10-simm-curvature.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-011](margin-collateral-im/11-simm-total-and-mpor.md).

- **Evidence — Approximate SIMM needs an independent portfolio and MPOR comparison.** Hand formulas and literal parameter transcriptions do not establish complete portfolio agreement or model-specific MPOR scaling. Obtain an independent licensed/reference comparison covering the implemented dimensional limitations, concentration and curvature aggregation. Cover high-concentration/mixed-sign CQ books, same-name multi-tenor and residual-plus-nonresidual credit, concentrated multi-name equity, FX dependence and mixed curvature factors/tenors/buckets. Independently verify all 289 commodity correlation cells and mixed-bucket vectors, including HVR diagnostic replay. Hand vectors do not settle omitted product dimensions or establish current-version/licensed SIMM certification. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-004](margin-collateral-im/04-simm-interest-rate.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-005](margin-collateral-im/05-simm-qualifying-credit.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-006](margin-collateral-im/06-simm-nonqualifying-credit.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-007](margin-collateral-im/07-simm-equity.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-008](margin-collateral-im/08-simm-fx.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-009](margin-collateral-im/09-simm-commodity.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-010](margin-collateral-im/10-simm-curvature.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-011](margin-collateral-im/11-simm-total-and-mpor.md).

- **Decision — Schedule and CCP margin leave rescaling and netting to callers.** Standalone outputs carry MPOR metadata without time scaling; portfolio callers rescale. Schedule trait defaults use five-year IR unless configured, and mixed-book NGR uses all supplied rows with a 40% floor. Agree legal netting partitions, maturity boundaries and applicable parameter versions. Adopt exact maturity-boundary, custom/missing-rate and all-negative/zero-gross-notional behavior, with model-versus-consumer MPOR provenance. Compare independent schedule portfolios and reverify the schedule version’s applicability. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-012](margin-collateral-im/12-schedule-notional.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-013](margin-collateral-im/13-schedule-net-gross.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-014](margin-collateral-im/14-ccp-external-and-proxy.md).

- **Decision — IM agreement elections and group allocation are separate from VM.** One-way IM threshold/MTA and custody flags are distinct from variation-margin rules; group allocation remains caller-owned. Confirm the actual agreement elections and responsibility for group or regulatory thresholds. Specify per-side account scope and consumer enforcement of segregation/timing; reconcile the unsupported InternalModel selection and distinguish model MPOR from contractual MPOR in result metadata. Constructor names alone do not establish legal eligibility or model approval. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-017](margin-collateral-im/17-csa-selection-and-im-terms.md).

- **Decision — VM replay models agreed collateral and does not enforce every schedule term.** VM uses symmetric thresholds, one additive nonnegative IA, MTA before rounding, delivery-up/return-down rounding, input-order replay and immediate agreed-collateral updates without frequency enforcement. Periodic/on-demand end boundaries differ. Agree pending versus settled scope, timing and legal elections. Include the lack of a post-round MTA recheck, nonnegative-IA domain, unsorted and same-day observation policy, calendar provenance and intraday terms. Qualify actual bilateral elections with independent call examples; unmapped currencies use weekend-only calendars. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-018](margin-collateral-im/18-variation-margin.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-019](margin-collateral-im/19-margin-dates-and-replay.md).

- **Decision — Collateral metrics are static proxies with special zero cases.** Utilization defines 0/0 as one and positive/zero as infinity; excess percentage is zero for nonpositive requirement. Haircut01 is positive collateral times one basis point and funding cost is undiscounted posted collateral times annual spread. Agree these result meanings without equating them to dynamic XVA or full-PV repricing. Adopt accepted negative-input domains and validation of externally supplied derived fields, as well as the Haircut01 sign/name; preserve those meanings in consuming reports. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-021](margin-collateral-im/21-collateral-metrics.md).

- **Evidence — Margin reference provenance needs qualification beyond the checked tables.** Historical source comparisons cover the specified CQ and commodity entries. Complete parameter coverage, the recorded historical PDF digest and independent portfolio and host qualification remain open. Inspected formula, transcription and transport fixtures retain their own evidence scope. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-001](margin-collateral-im/01-registry-and-defaults.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-002](margin-collateral-im/02-simm-parameter-validation.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-022](margin-collateral-im/22-wire-tables-and-provenance.md).

- **Investigate — Margin aggregation under extreme and offsetting inputs.** Quantify overflow, cancellation and insertion-order effects in ordinary sensitivity sums, concentration-adjusted SIMM quadratics and schedule gross-by-class/notional totals. Include large cancelling IR/FX inputs and floored quadratic sums; deterministic serialization alone does not establish numerical accuracy or invariance. Qualify affected outputs against independent scaled/offsetting cases before drawing an impact conclusion. Records: [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-003](margin-collateral-im/03-sensitivity-inputs.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-004](margin-collateral-im/04-simm-interest-rate.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-008](margin-collateral-im/08-simm-fx.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-010](margin-collateral-im/10-simm-curvature.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-011](margin-collateral-im/11-simm-total-and-mpor.md), [MARGIN-COLLATERAL-IM-013](margin-collateral-im/13-schedule-net-gross.md).

## Statements: model, formula and check contracts

[Scope inventory](statements-contracts/README.md).

- **Reconciliation — Bundled reported-cost earnings and margins.** Reconcile the bundled earnings, margins and cost ratios, including signed expenses, known-zero and missing dependencies, revenue eligibility and recomputation from aggregated inputs. Qualify the D&A inclusion and single add-back with an independent accounting example across formulas, examples, bindings and fixtures. Until then a bundled ratio whose denominator is exactly zero fails the run with a division-by-zero error, where it used to publish no value; a missing denominator still leaves the ratio unavailable. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-007](statements-contracts/07-earnings-and-margins.md#accepted-decision-for-reported-cost-earnings-and-margins).

- **Reconciliation — Typed capital selectors in Statements formulas.** Replace the `cs.<component>.<instrument or total>` reference form with the typed capital reporting components and selectors, including instrument, native-currency total and consolidated scopes, and qualify each selector through parsing, rendering, compilation and evaluation. The rest of the formula grammar, signature and AST-validation contract is reconciled. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-004](statements-contracts/04-dsl-syntax-and-lowering.md#accepted-decision-for-formula-grammar-function-dispatch-and-ast-validation) · [Capital reporting and selectors](statements-capital/09-reporting-and-fx.md#accepted-decision-for-capital-reporting-and-fx).

- **Reconciliation — Typed forecasts and staged validation.** Implement the common typed forecast specification, explicit method controls, complete static validation and runtime checks for the eligible horizon and history. Qualify build/load, inactive configurations, units, peer compatibility and exact stochastic seed round-trips in Rust, Python, WASM and JSON. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-003](statements-contracts/03-forecast-parameter-contract.md#accepted-decision-for-typed-forecasts-and-validation-staging).

- **Reconciliation — Capital reference units in Statements formulas.** Resolve `cs` references through the accepted instrument-native, native-currency total and consolidated reporting scopes, and delete the unresolved unit a formula may still carry when a capital reference has no resolved currency; a required missing quote makes consolidated reporting unavailable. Qualify missing required FX across hosts. The unit rules, monetary-variance rejection, resolution of every other unit before execution and exact currency-tagged monetary results are reconciled. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-005](statements-contracts/05-currency-dimensions.md#accepted-decision-for-formula-units-and-currency) · [Capital reporting and FX](statements-capital/09-reporting-and-fx.md#accepted-decision-for-capital-reporting-and-fx).

- **Reconciliation — Metric registration, pinned dependencies and atomic model import.** Enforce atomic catalogue registration, pinned local and qualified bindings, AST-only qualification, deterministic historical closure and checked same-period dependencies. Qualify shared templates, conflicting imports and explicit overrides. The builder now uses stored namespaces and has a dotted-namespace regression. Its textual rewriter can still collide with function names. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-006](statements-contracts/06-registry-and-dependencies.md#accepted-decision-for-metric-registration-and-dependencies).

- **Reconciliation — Bounded Statements wire admission, schemas and errors.** Implement one loading path with exact version and numeric admission, shared limits and runnable validation before canonical content hashing. Retain separate evaluation provenance, validate canonical instrument eligibility and reconcile structured errors across hosts. Correct the empty schema fixture and ordering description, and independently qualify Rust, Python and WASM parity, boundaries and replay. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-016](statements-contracts/16-wire-schema-and-errors.md#accepted-decision-for-statements-wire-admission-schemas-and-errors).

- **Reconciliation — Bundled leverage and coverage definitions.** Use period-end gross debt and reported-cost EBITDA, complete TTM and positive denominators, statement interest including recognized PIK for interest coverage, and contractual due-date scheduled cash service for debt-service coverage. Qualify scope, currency, denominators and due-date service independently across formulas, examples, bindings and fixtures. Remove generic thresholds and require agreement-specific covenant terms. Until then a bundled ratio whose denominator is exactly zero fails the run with a division-by-zero error, where it used to publish no value; a missing denominator still leaves the ratio unavailable. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-008](statements-contracts/08-leverage-and-coverage.md#accepted-decision-for-bundled-leverage-and-coverage).

- **Reconciliation — Bundled period returns on comparable average capital.** Use shared reported-cost earnings, comparable opening and closing balances, explicit operating tax and invested capital for ROIC, and reasoned availability with positive denominators. Reconcile the separate audited `taxes` lookup to shared statement `tax_expense` for net income and explicit operating tax for ROIC. Independently qualify reporting scope, operating capital, tax credits, signed losses and coarser-period recomputation across formulas, examples, bindings and fixtures. Until then a bundled ratio whose denominator is exactly zero fails the run with a division-by-zero error, where it used to publish no value; a missing denominator still leaves the ratio unavailable. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-009](statements-contracts/09-returns-on-capital.md#accepted-decision-for-bundled-period-returns-on-capital).

- **Reconciliation — Complete balance-sheet articulation at every model close.** Enforce disjoint compatible account groups, checked accumulation, signed and absolute residuals, and explicit allowance with zero default. Report missing node IDs and require all periods to be complete and within tolerance to pass. Independently qualify account-set validation, cancellation, optional relative residuals, and exact tolerance boundaries. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-010](statements-contracts/10-balance-sheet-articulation.md#accepted-decision-for-balance-sheet-articulation).

- **Reconciliation — Cash roll-forward and independent component reconciliation.** Enforce a dated first opening, adjacent closes, sourced signed adjustments, and all-or-none distinct classified cash-flow components. Require complete comparisons from the first period, checked arithmetic, zero default tolerance and unfloored reference amounts. Independently qualify adjustment scope, missing/invalid classification and arithmetic across reporting scales. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-011](statements-contracts/11-cash-reconciliation.md#accepted-decision-for-cash-roll-forward-and-component-reconciliation).

- **Reconciliation — Complete retained-earnings reconciliation.** Enforce dated opening and adjacent balances, same-scope net income, accepted dividend signs and sourced direct retained-earnings adjustments. Qualify restatements, duplicate prevention, checked arithmetic, the unfloored tolerance reference and complete comparisons independently. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-012](statements-contracts/12-retained-earnings-reconciliation.md#accepted-decision-for-retained-earnings-reconciliation).

- **Reconciliation — Statements data-quality coverage and validity.** Enforce explicit required scope, incomplete required observations, valid typed zero, reasoned unavailability and invalid-input errors on present optional cells. Qualify identifiers, off-plan cells, compatible units, empty coverage and advisory finite sign groups. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-013](statements-contracts/13-data-quality-checks.md#accepted-decision-for-data-quality-coverage-and-validity).

- **Reconciliation — Numerical comparison and check-suite outcomes.** Implement typed comparisons with zero default identity tolerance, unfiltered coverage verdicts and advisory-only filters. Enforce check order, identity, merge compatibility and verified supplied-result provenance. Independently qualify compared precision, residual scales, absolute and relative tolerance, empty coverage, filtering and requested-context evaluation. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-014](statements-contracts/14-comparison-and-suite-policy.md#accepted-decision-for-numerical-comparisons-and-check-suites).

- **Reconciliation — Explicit formula-check modes, eligibility and lazy evaluation.** Enforce Boolean predicates and typed residuals, actual/forecast scope, complete AST validation and lazy observation consumption on a verified run. Independently qualify untaken branches and explicit fallback, eligibility, typed tolerance, missing coverage and execution errors even for advisory checks. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-015](statements-contracts/15-formula-checks.md#accepted-decision-for-formula-checks-and-coverage).

## Statements: evaluation, forecasts and reporting

[Scope inventory](statements-evaluation/README.md).

- **Reconciliation — Capital-state orchestration, dependency validation and atomic period snapshots.** Establish dated obligations, enforce transitive input independence and allocate once per period. Finalize reporting with explicit period-close FX, then evaluate downstream formulas using the same forecast shocks and commit period data together. Independently qualify multi-period cash and debt bridges, issuance boundaries, downstream evaluation and replay. [Accepted STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-012](statements-evaluation/12-capital-state-orchestration.md#accepted-decision-for-capital-state-orchestration-and-period-snapshots).

- **Reconciliation — Explicit period rollup, typed accumulation and provenance.** Require an explicit method for each rolled node and required input, complete canonical coarser periods, checked Money or compensated scalar sums, final-cell status for PeriodEnd, target-cadence formulas and traceable source runs. Reconcile the audited automatic Sum and ordinary floating-point accumulation. Independently qualify coverage, cancellation across scales, flow, balance and ratio examples, cadence and unavailable inputs. [Accepted STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-014](statements-evaluation/14-period-rollup.md#accepted-decision-for-period-rollup).

- **Reconciliation — Aligned trend history, checked fitted levels and extrapolation.** Implement centered and scaled OLS over reporting slots, damped Holt and centered moving-average forecasts on eligible histories. Independently qualify history alignment, parameter boundaries, large levels, cancellation, representability and replay before bounds. [Accepted STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-009](statements-evaluation/09-trend-extrapolation.md#accepted-decision-for-trend-extrapolation).

- **Reconciliation — Latent one-peer correlation, ordering and replay.** Enforce positive-volatility Monte Carlo peers, zero-coefficient independence, exact endpoints and a stable interior mix. Qualify deterministic peer ordering and cycles, canonical slot matching through masks and explicit values, chained shocks and replay. The live generator shares the independent seed mixer and still requires peer lookup at zero correlation. [Accepted STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-008](statements-evaluation/08-correlated-shocks.md#accepted-decision-for-latent-one-peer-correlation).

- **Reconciliation — Ordered normalization bridges and separately named adjusted metrics.** Validate references, units and canonical period keys before calculation. Enforce signed adjustments, individual caps and checked arithmetic for every bridge row. Replace existing-node overwrite with collision-checked publication and source provenance. Independently qualify unavailable inputs, Reported and Progressive caps, intermediate overflow with a finite final total, D&A add-backs, and explicit adjusted references in ratios and covenant checks. [Accepted STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-015](statements-evaluation/15-normalization-adjustments.md#accepted-decision-for-normalization-bridges).

- **Reconciliation — Complete Monte Carlo paths, typed samples and exact quantiles.** Implement the path-ID and resource limits, fresh complete paths with deterministic failure order, explicit metric coverage and typed sample validation. Qualify R-7, exact percentile keys, sample count and units, complete path restoration and finite typed strict-upper breach probability. Establish independent moment, quantile and sampling-error evidence. [Accepted STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-011](statements-evaluation/11-monte-carlo-evaluation.md#accepted-decision-for-monte-carlo-paths-quantiles-and-breach-probabilities).

- **Reconciliation — Checked stochastic forecast kernels and replay.** Implement signed normal and mean-reverting steps, unclipped positive lognormal restoration, explicit growth or difference bootstrap with unbiased draws, zero-volatility transitions without draws, and versioned PCG64 streams. Independently qualify moments, extremes, subnormals, bootstrap indices, seed provenance and replay. [Accepted STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-007](statements-evaluation/07-stochastic-forecast-kernels.md#accepted-decision-for-stochastic-forecast-kernels-and-replay).

- **Reconciliation — Canonical historical windows, ranks, cumulative functions and EWM.** Implement exact model-slot windows, explicit sample coverage, exact rank ties, complete prefixes and slot-based decay with Boolean correction controls. Independently qualify missing observations, sample counts, R-7, monetary units and sums, and corrected EWM at weight-degeneracy boundaries. [Accepted STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-004](statements-evaluation/04-historical-statistics-and-ewm.md#accepted-decision-for-historical-statistics-and-ewm).

- **Reconciliation — Canonical forecast suffix, anchoring and output bounds.** Implement one suffix per node and run/path, explicit starting levels and eligible immediate anchors by method, and aligned supplied history. Qualify missing inputs, masked and explicit slots, keyed override carry and clipping provenance while preserving raw recurrence, phase and shocks. [Accepted STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-005](statements-evaluation/05-forecast-segments-and-bounds.md#accepted-decision-for-forecast-segments-anchoring-and-output-bounds).

- **Reconciliation — Deterministic forecast levels, keyed carry and fades.** Enforce signed per-slot growth, canonical keyed overrides with unavailable leading slots, checked signed convex and log-magnitude fades, and finite half-life and empty-horizon validation. Independently qualify sign changes and extreme endpoints, including representable geometric paths whose endpoint ratio overflows or underflows. [Accepted STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-006](statements-evaluation/06-deterministic-forecasts-and-fades.md#accepted-decision-for-deterministic-forecasts-and-fades).

- **Reconciliation — Aligned seasonal decomposition and phase-preserving projection.** Implement checked centered averages and unsnapped edge slopes, zero-mean additive effects and normalized multiplicative factors. Qualify equal-period histories and phase, signed trends with zero observations, extreme factors and output bounds. [Accepted STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-010](statements-evaluation/10-seasonal-decomposition.md#accepted-decision-for-seasonal-decomposition-and-projection).

- **Reconciliation — The check suite at single-run finalization.** Apply the configured check suite at single-run finalization under the accepted comparison and suite policy, and qualify configured single-run checks. Cell states, mask handling, the lag and shift dependency rule, source precedence under a cutoff, checked plan reuse and published run provenance with each cell's status, reason and source are reconciled. [Accepted STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-001](statements-evaluation/01-graph-precedence-and-prepared-plans.md#accepted-decision-for-dependency-graphs-cell-precedence-and-prepared-evaluation) · [Check-suite policy](statements-contracts/14-comparison-and-suite-policy.md#accepted-decision-for-numerical-comparisons-and-check-suites).

- **Reconciliation — Float views of typed values inside Statements.** Typed cells and typed formula values are authoritative, but these internal consumers still read a float view in which a value that is missing, masked or unavailable is `NaN`: the statistical history kernels (rolling, cumulative, exponentially weighted and full-history statistics, `rank`, `quantile`) and capital-structure references, whose results re-enter formulas as an untyped number that takes the units static analysis fixed; formula checks, period rollup and inline waterfall expressions (`evaluate_formula`); normalization bridges and the non-finite data-quality check (`StatementResult::float_series`); and Monte Carlo path collection (`StatementResult::path_values`). Move each to typed values and delete its view when its owning contract is reconciled. [Historical statistics](statements-evaluation/04-historical-statistics-and-ewm.md) · [Formula checks](statements-contracts/15-formula-checks.md) · [Period rollup](statements-evaluation/14-period-rollup.md) · [Waterfall allocation](statements-capital/06-priority-allocation.md) · [Normalization bridges](statements-evaluation/15-normalization-adjustments.md) · [Data-quality checks](statements-contracts/13-data-quality-checks.md) · [Monte Carlo paths](statements-evaluation/11-monte-carlo-evaluation.md) · [Capital reporting](statements-capital/09-reporting-and-fx.md).

- **Reconciliation — Monetary rounding mode in Statements formulas.** `round(money, digits)` states its scale and rounds midpoint ties away from zero, the one mode the function has; `floor`, `ceil` and one-argument `round` reject money when the model is built. The accepted contract requires monetary rounding to state its rounding mode as well as its scale. Add the mode to the formula grammar, or accept the single documented mode. [Accepted STATEMENTS-EVALUATION-002](statements-evaluation/02-scalar-formulas-and-undefined-values.md#accepted-decision-for-scalar-formulas-and-unavailable-values) · [Monetary rounding](core-conventions/012-money-decimal-precision.md#accepted-decision-monetary-precision-rounding-and-currency-identity).

## Statements analytics: valuation, comparisons and templates

[Scope inventory](statements-analytics-valuation/README.md).

- **Reconciliation — Statements analytics evaluations read the full snapshot.** Corporate valuation, the valuation orchestrator and scenario-set evaluation pass `ObservationCutoff::FullSnapshot` to the Statements evaluator, so `as_of` dates pricing and no longer hides unpublished observations. Expose the observation cutoff on these entry points and their hosts, and qualify a dated replay against an identified snapshot. [Accepted STATEMENTS-CONTRACTS-002](statements-contracts/02-authoring-and-availability.md#accepted-decision-for-authoring-imports-and-observation-availability).

- **Reconciliation — Statements analytics reads typed cells through a float view.** Analytics reads every statement cell through one crate-private float view (`cell_float`) in which a missing, masked or unavailable cell is `NaN` and money is its nearest float, which preserves the behaviour it had before typed results. Read status, reason and exact amounts from the typed cell at each analytics boundary and delete the view. [Typed results](statements-evaluation/13-result-units-and-exports.md#accepted-decision-for-typed-results-and-exports).

- **Reconciliation — Statement cashflows and the corporate DCF bridge.** Require an authored valuation date, snapshot and cutoff, explicit native currency, complete dated FCFF, a qualified terminal basis and a same-date equity bridge. Reconcile WACC admission, pricing-owner results and replayable provenance. Independently qualify cutoff and bridge dates, annual basis, mixed-frequency and leap-year timing, WACC and tax assumptions, terminal selection, and enterprise-to-equity reconciliation. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-01](statements-analytics-valuation/01-corporate-dcf.md#accepted-decision-for-statements-corporate-dcf).

- **Reconciliation — Verified DCF sensitivity baseline and independent shocks.** Reuse the admitted corporate DCF context and record explicit parameter-specific shocks. Reconcile automatic clamps and the audited H-model co-shock with individual endpoint availability, preserve valid opposite endpoints, and distinguish malformed or failed pricing from rejected levels. Independently qualify market-dependent baseline reuse, represented levels, asymmetric ranges, domain boundaries, complete-pair ranking and separation from `dcf::wacc01`. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-02](statements-analytics-valuation/02-dcf-sensitivity.md#accepted-decision-for-statements-dcf-sensitivity).

- **Reconciliation — Dated LBO transaction, sponsor funding and exit equity.** Validate dates and matched earnings bases, reconcile seller consideration and issued debt proceeds separately from face, and compare supplied funding with uses in checked Money. Preserve the signed exit equity residual, apply the common equity proceeds floor and distinguish funded MOIC from sizing. Independently qualify date and earnings coverage, commitments and funding gaps, exit claims, returns and required-check gating on the verified run. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-03](statements-analytics-valuation/03-lbo.md#accepted-decision-for-statements-lbo-funding-and-returns).

- **Reconciliation — Explicit corporate-analysis selection, check coverage and typed reports.** Reconcile builder ordering and the audited check-name gate with a checked final request and verified results for all paths. Enforce period, currency and debt-scope matching for LTV and debt-to-EV, complete TTM leverage and coverage, checked ratio operands and consistent reporting of signed FCF and unavailable values. Independently qualify supplied checks and blocked outputs, mixed-frequency and leap-year windows, and typed Money, actual currency and scale across tables and text. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-04](statements-analytics-valuation/04-analysis-reports-units.md#accepted-decision-for-corporate-analysis-and-typed-reporting).

- **Reconciliation — Comparable-company admission, matched metrics and peer statistics.** Enforce unique peer IDs, subject exclusion across paths, finite currency-qualified screening, time and basis matching, metric units and usable coverage. Reconcile the audited population and domain gaps. Independently qualify filter exclusions, signed and unavailable inputs, ties, R-7 quantiles, sample dispersion, constant-response fits and extrapolation with ranked-data and regression reference vectors. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-05](statements-analytics-valuation/05-comps-population-statistics.md#accepted-decision-for-comparable-company-populations-and-statistics).

- **Reconciliation — Comparable-company signals, composite weight coverage and fit diagnostics.** Require explicit unique dimensions and directions, separate raw diagnostics from sample-residual scores, and report extrapolated fit details with unavailable scores. Enforce default complete coverage and explicit partial-weight thresholds. Report usable peers and defined weighted regression R². Independently qualify normalization, directions, duplicates, unavailable dimensions, weight coverage and composite arithmetic. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-06](statements-analytics-valuation/06-comps-scoring.md#accepted-decision-for-comparable-company-scoring).

- **Reconciliation — Selected forecast goal-seek assumptions, checked search and typed verification.** Enforce selected editable forecast drivers, fixed run context, explicit bracket or bounded search, and a fresh exact-typed target check with separately named tolerances. The live fallible callback carries driver-conversion and evaluation errors. Reconcile implicit retries and independently qualify typed monetary trials, evaluation counts, discontinuities, nonmonotone and multiple-root cases, and replay. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-07](statements-analytics-valuation/07-goal-seek.md#accepted-decision-for-statements-goal-seek).

- **Reconciliation — As-issued forecast pairs, checked errors and per-metric coverage.** Enforce matched target periods, units and snapshot provenance, explicit partial coverage, scaled arithmetic and separate exact-zero policies and counts for MAPE and sMAPE. Reconcile the audited scale-dependent exclusions and missing coverage details. Independently qualify signed and extreme-scale pairs, missing and duplicate pairs, host and wire consistency, and out-of-sample assessed cases. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-08](statements-analytics-valuation/08-forecast-errors.md#accepted-decision-for-forecast-error-pairing-and-metrics).

- **Reconciliation — Validated scenario inheritance, fresh runs and explicit typed baselines.** Reconcile separate-map precedence and inferred comparisons with root-to-child cell resolution, complete specification replacement, stable evaluation context and an explicit compatible baseline. Independently qualify superseded override validation, child-wide and period-specific interactions, selected masks and forecast values, run provenance, missing periods and signed comparisons at zero baselines. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-09](statements-analytics-valuation/09-scenario-inheritance.md#accepted-decision-for-scenario-inheritance-and-comparisons).

- **Reconciliation — Verified sensitivity baselines, bounded grids and tested-response tornadoes.** Enforce baseline agreement, typed levels and checked work limits. Validate independent case state, deterministic ordering and unavailability reporting. Reconcile input-endpoint selection and cross-unit ranking with the accepted single-target, single-period tested-response span. Independently qualify interior extrema, completeness and ties, zero percentage bases and resource boundaries. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-10](statements-analytics-valuation/10-sensitivity-grids.md#accepted-decision-for-statement-sensitivity-grids-and-tornado-reports).

- **Reconciliation — Matched variance inputs and explicitly signed additive bridges.** Enforce matched metadata and run provenance, side-specific cell statuses, checked arithmetic and exact-zero relative denominators. Reconcile the audited raw-delta bridge with explicitly signed, ordered and disjoint components. Independently qualify positive and negative directions, invalid overlap and duplicate selection, incomplete bridges and signed remainder reconciliation. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-11](statements-analytics-valuation/11-variance-bridges.md#accepted-decision-for-statement-variances-and-additive-bridges).

- **Reconciliation — Validated dependency traversal and original-evaluation explanations.** Expose labeled graph relationships, unique and displayed references, and truncation. Reconcile assumed provenance and current-period fallback with verified cell sources, executed branches, historical windows and capital reads. Independently qualify additive grouping and repeated terms, incomplete evidence, nonlinear operands, exact money and matching host diagnostics. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-12](statements-analytics-valuation/12-dependency-explanations.md#accepted-decision-for-dependency-traversal-and-formula-explanations).

- **Reconciliation — Typed account roll-forwards, opening continuity and explicit articulation.** Reconcile the audited tolerance, identity membership and lenient status with typed movements and comparisons, dated first openings, separate continuity and closing checks, and complete required verdicts. Reuse the accepted balance-sheet articulation and suite contracts. Independently qualify scope and overlap, boundary adjustments, exact monetary residuals, tolerance references, incomplete inputs and advisory versus required gating. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-13](statements-analytics-valuation/13-corkscrew.md#accepted-decision-for-account-roll-forwards-and-articulation).

- **Reconciliation — Verified credit-scorecard inputs, explicit bands and coverage gates.** Reuse Core 019's implemented caller-supplied scale snapshot and strict label validation. Implement verified target inputs, typed scoreable domains and bands, complete or expressly partial weight coverage, and grade suppression or gating by verdict. Independently qualify boundary and weight cases, run provenance, partial grades, failed runs, complete minimum gates and the internal policy's empirical basis where claimed. Until then a scorecard metric formula that divides by zero fails the scorecard, where it used to exclude the factor; an unavailable metric is still excluded. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-14](statements-analytics-valuation/14-credit-scorecards.md#accepted-decision-for-credit-scorecard-bands-and-coverage).

- **Reconciliation — Validated roll-forward templates and complete vintage coefficients.** Validate template inputs and node insertion, typed openings, signed movements and immediately preceding closes. Replace the audited coefficient cutoff and missing-history fallback with exact-zero handling and explicit cohort coverage. Independently qualify required and masked movements, historical calendar coverage, typed zero, small coefficients at large scales, checked arithmetic and host parity. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-15](statements-analytics-valuation/15-recurrence-templates.md#accepted-decision-for-roll-forward-and-vintage-templates).

- **Reconciliation — Validated lease timing, expected renewal attribution and typed property statements.** Reuse Core calendar slots and reconcile whole-period timing, explicit growth, renewal and concession attribution, and single-currency property statements. Validate duplicate leases, overlapping inputs and output collisions before inserting nodes. Independently qualify calendar and annual basis, timing and lease-phase boundaries, expected component reconciliation at monetary precision, management-fee bases, missing inputs and insertion failures using independent lease examples. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-VALUATION-16](statements-analytics-valuation/16-real-estate-templates.md#accepted-decision-for-lease-projections-and-property-statements).

## Statements analytics: credit checks and impairment

[Scope inventory](statements-analytics-credit/README.md).

- **Reconciliation — Explicit mapped check selection, caller thresholds and required coverage.** Enforce full three-statement mapping and explicitly elected supplemental and credit checks, label tested series, preserve caller LBO thresholds and validate selected inputs including cash and burn. Reconcile audited sign warnings, zero substitution for missing movements and omission of working-capital comparison rows. Independently qualify cash-versus-accrual bridges, dated PPE openings and disposal carrying values, dividend-payable timing, adjusted working capital and tolerance boundaries. - **Reconciliation — Direct interest recognition and separately elected advisory estimates.** Require verified scope, currency, sign and recognition, with cash-pay interest, PIK and explicitly mapped adjustments. Require complete dated outstanding balances and quoted rate conventions for an estimate. Reconcile the audited skips, partial-pair sums and current-balance substitution. Independently qualify accrual and settlement timing, irregular periods, annual-rate conversion, missing prior balances, checked units and typed tolerance boundaries. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-03](statements-analytics-credit/03-interest-reconciliation.md#accepted-decision-for-interest-reconciliation-and-estimates).

- **Reconciliation — Signed period-change screening on verified adjacent periods.** Apply checked signed changes relative to prior magnitude, preserve a signed difference at an exact-zero base and admit tiny nonzero observations. Require explicit transition scope, compatible units and sourced inclusive period bounds. Independently qualify signed examples, zero and tiny priors, scope and adjacency, breach versus gating, incomplete inputs and typed diagnostics. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-04](statements-analytics-credit/04-growth-plausibility.md#accepted-decision-for-signed-period-change-relative-to-prior-magnitude).

- **Reconciliation — Informational effective tax rate with explicit scope and coverage.** Use signed recognized tax expense, matching units and reporting scope, a strictly positive pretax denominator, and sourced inclusive informational bounds. Independently qualify charges and benefits, exact-zero and loss reasons, missing and invalid inputs, actual and forecast scope, and the unevaluated verdict. - **Reconciliation — Bundled TTM leverage and coverage with explicit thresholds.** Implement aligned recognized-interest and scheduled-service denominators, preserve known zero and signed outcomes where applicable, and require named custom definitions for other windows. Reconcile the audited current-period coverage and unusable trailing sums with typed per-period coverage and sourced nested thresholds. Independently qualify complete supported calendars, invalid and incomplete windows, denominator domains, threshold equality and precedence, and custom checks. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-06](statements-analytics-credit/06-leverage-and-coverage.md#accepted-decision-for-bundled-ttm-leverage-and-coverage-checks).

- **Reconciliation — Verified spendable cash, signed cash use and reporting-month runway.** Reconcile the monthly burn mapping, signed cash-use and shortfall cases, cross-node units, finite arithmetic and the timing of calendar validation with the accepted runway contract. Independently qualify calendar slots and unsupported periods, flat cash use and generation, immediate shortfalls, incomplete inputs and sourced threshold order and equality. - **Reconciliation — Negative-FCF streaks and adverse trends with verified continuity.** Apply explicit named series and directions, ordered FCF thresholds and strict adjacent trend transitions with validated cadence and units. Reconcile the audited level-trend description, silent eligibility gaps and invalid inputs. Independently qualify threshold equality and continuing findings, reset and carry at the forecast boundary, missing and masked observations, and confirmed-count and coverage reporting. - **Reconciliation — Economic principal, seniority ratios and final maturity reporting.** Reconcile currency identity in summary exports, FX provenance, eligible cash for signed net debt, and recognized borrowing interest in through-rank coverage. Preserve native results and unaffected ratios when coverage or FX is unavailable. Independently qualify verified fiscal and custom-period windows, class assignments and pari-passu ratios, missing-interest propagation, positive gross shares, and the final maturity wall alongside dated principal claims. - **Reconciliation — Verified instrument ratios, dated collateral and complete path reporting.** Enforce one eligible borrowing, selected measures and calendar periods tied to the same statement and capital run. Reconcile scheduled cash service, the selected PIK and fee variants, recognized interest, period-end face and matched reference values. Independently qualify unknown instrument and duplicate references, zero service, missing rows, same-scope typed operands and currency, collateral coverage independent of service, and observed and full-path minima. Reconcile the audited grid-dependent forecast anchor and midpoint approximations with fixed-lookback hazard, survival continuity and grid boundaries. Enforce explicit discount and recovery choices and preserve bucket and scenario evidence. Independently qualify immediate and linear integrals, arbitrary PD and EAD knots, invalid and limiting PDs, zero maturity or forecast, extreme rates and recovery delays, and positive-weight scenario contributions. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-17](statements-analytics-credit/17-cecl-forecast-reversion.md#accepted-decision-for-lifetime-cecl-forecast-and-reversion).

- **Reconciliation — Explicit impairment exposure and cumulative default inputs.** Require selected EAD basis, explicit CCF when applicable, sourced LGD, reporting and snapshot clocks, and anchored and covered schedules or elected tails. Reconcile audited scalar exposure metadata, default CCF, flat tail and conditional probability at exhausted survival. Independently qualify total and drawn EAD, CCF provenance, opening coverage, negative discount rates, maturity boundaries, interpolation and tails, exact rating keys, and interval mass and conditional probability for raw and custom sources. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-12](statements-analytics-credit/12-ecl-exposure-and-pd.md#accepted-decision-for-exposure-and-cumulative-default-inputs).

- **Reconciliation — Explicit immutable impairment policy revisions and replay provenance.** Require a complete direct policy or an exact caller-registry revision, and validate method parameters, effective-date and cutoff eligibility, scope and parameter provenance through one fallible path. Reconcile embedded defaults and divergent validation with resolved snapshots, separately versioned overrides and content identity. Independently qualify revision eligibility, override resolution, parameter calibration evidence, host parity and replay. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-14](statements-analytics-credit/14-ecl-policy-defaults.md#accepted-decision-for-resolved-impairment-policy-snapshots).

- **Reconciliation — Matched-window impairment staging and dated cure observations.** Reconcile the audited horizon cap, zero-origination relative error, label handling and generic cure flag with matched dated PD evidence, explicit thresholds, default priority and two separate cure windows. Independently qualify identical rating labels across snapshots, conditional origination PD, zero baselines, threshold equality, incomplete stage-changing evidence and probation interruptions. Qualify institution-specific SICR and cure policy. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-13](statements-analytics-credit/13-ecl-staging.md#accepted-decision-for-impairment-staging-and-cure).

- **Reconciliation — Stage-specific impairment horizons, knot-aligned buckets and dated recovery.** Use a verified stage and selected policy, unconditional default mass, explicit LGD, an exact horizon and checked compensated amounts. Reconcile Stage 3 admission to carrying exposure and dated recovery inputs, remove its audited PD dependency, and retain typed currency and provenance. Independently qualify custom PD consumption and elected tails, Stage 1 full associated LGD, zero horizons, knot and scale behavior at a stated tolerance, and the signed discounted recovery shortfall. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-15](statements-analytics-credit/15-ecl-bucket-measurement.md#accepted-decision-for-stage-specific-impairment-measurement).

- **Reconciliation — Exact impairment scenario weights and complete positive-weight measurement.** Reconcile audited weight tolerance, duplicate IDs, zero-weight pricing and convenience defaults with shared verified inputs, exact probabilities, and per-scenario PD, LGD or recovery evidence. Require checked weighted arithmetic and replayable typed output. Independently qualify one-scenario parity, different PD grids, missing positive-weight evidence, zero-weight metadata, LGD modes, dated recoveries and policy and input provenance. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-16](statements-analytics-credit/16-ecl-scenario-weighting.md#accepted-decision-for-weighted-impairment-scenarios).

- **Reconciliation — WARM performing pools, sourced loss rates and knot-aligned exposure-years.** Enforce verified eligibility, separate impaired recovery assessment, a sourced exposure-matched annual net loss fraction and explicit treatment of incompatible settings. Reconcile the audited midpoint approximation with checked integration through every knot and the exact horizon. Independently qualify pool and loss-rate provenance, adjustments, incomplete eligibility evidence, arbitrary knot placement, zero and long lifetimes, dynamic range, and allowances above opening exposure. [Accepted STATEMENTS-ANALYTICS-CREDIT-18](statements-analytics-credit/18-cecl-warm.md#accepted-decision-for-warm-exposure-years-and-annual-loss-rates).

- **Reconciliation — Verified statement covenant observations and read-only assessments.** Replace all-period raw-metric delegation with explicit terms, typed mappings, selected periods and verified provenance. Preserve stored period boundaries and test on the final included day for period-end assessments. Distinguish applicability from assessment, missing data and breaches. With the covenant engine owners, independently qualify fiscal boundaries, springing and waived tests, metric precision, projected periods, effective terms, explicit volatility, breach probabilities, empty-list coverage and read-only history access.
